Conclusion: WaveSFNet通过小波编解码器和空间-频率双域门控翻译器的统一设计，有效解决了时空预测中长程动态建模与高频细节保留的平衡问题，在保持高效性的同时提升了预测性能。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal predictive learning aims to forecast future frames from historical observations in an unsupervised manner, and is critical to a wide range of applications. The key challenge is to model long-range dynamics while preserving high-frequency details for sharp multi-step predictions. Existing efficient recurrent-free frameworks typically rely on strided convolutions or pooling for sampling, which tends to discard textures and boundaries, while purely spatial operators often struggle to balance local interactions with global propagation. To address these issues, we propose WaveSFNet, an efficient framework that unifies a wavelet-based codec with a spatial--frequency dual-domain gated spatiotemporal translator. The wavelet-based codec preserves high-frequency subband cues during downsampling and reconstruction. Meanwhile, the translator first injects adjacent-frame differences to explicitly enhance dynamic information, and then performs dual-domain gated fusion between large-kernel spatial local modeling and frequency-domain global modulation, together with gated channel interaction for cross-channel feature exchange. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WaveSFNet achieves competitive prediction accuracy on Moving MNIST, TaxiBJ, and WeatherBench, while maintaining low computational complexity. Our code is available at https://github.com/fhjdqaq/WaveSFNet.

</details>


### [113] [Drop-In Perceptual Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23297)
*Ezgi Ozyilkan,Zhiqi Chen,Oren Rippel,Jona Ballé,Kedar Tatwawadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统探索了3D高斯泼溅的感知优化策略，发现正则化Wasserstein Distortion损失（WD-R）能显著提升渲染质量，在人类主观评价中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D高斯泼溅方法的输出最终由人类观看，但现有方法通常依赖像素级损失的临时组合，导致渲染结果模糊。需要系统探索感知优化策略来提升3DGS的视觉质量。

Method: 对多种失真损失进行系统搜索，首次进行大规模人类主观研究（39,320对评分），评估不同数据集和3DGS框架。提出正则化Wasserstein Distortion损失（WD-R），并在Mip-Splatting和Scaffold-GS等框架中验证其泛化能力。

Result: WD-R在人类主观评价中表现最佳：相比原始3DGS损失被偏好2.3倍，相比当前最佳方法Perceptual-GS被偏好1.5倍。在LPIPS、DISTS和FID指标上达到SOTA，在Mip-Splatting和Scaffold-GS框架中分别被偏好1.8倍和3.6倍。在3DGS场景压缩任务中，可比感知指标性能下节省约50%比特率。

Conclusion: WD-R是3D高斯泼溅感知优化的有效损失函数，能显著提升视觉质量而不增加计算资源，具有良好的泛化性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Despite their output being ultimately consumed by human viewers, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods often rely on ad-hoc combinations of pixel-level losses, resulting in blurry renderings. To address this, we systematically explore perceptual optimization strategies for 3DGS by searching over a diverse set of distortion losses. We conduct the first-of-its-kind large-scale human subjective study on 3DGS, involving 39,320 pairwise ratings across several datasets and 3DGS frameworks. A regularized version of Wasserstein Distortion, which we call WD-R, emerges as the clear winner, excelling at recovering fine textures without incurring a higher splat count. WD-R is preferred by raters more than $2.3\times$ over the original 3DGS loss, and $1.5\times$ over current best method Perceptual-GS. WD-R also consistently achieves state-of-the-art LPIPS, DISTS, and FID scores across various datasets, and generalizes across recent frameworks, such as Mip-Splatting and Scaffold-GS, where replacing the original loss with WD-R consistently enhances perceptual quality within a similar resource budget (number of splats for Mip-Splatting, model size for Scaffold-GS), and leads to reconstructions being preferred by human raters $1.8\times$ and $3.6\times$, respectively. We also find that this carries over to the task of 3DGS scene compression, with $\approx 50\%$ bitrate savings for comparable perceptual metric performance.

</details>


### [114] [ARGENT: Adaptive Hierarchical Image-Text Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23311)
*Chuong Huynh,Hossein Souri,Abhinav Kumar,Vitali Petsiuk,Deen Dayal Mohan,Suren Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARGENT提出了一种自适应蕴含损失和范数正则化器，解决了双曲视觉语言模型中蕴含锥崩溃问题，并引入了基于角度的概率蕴含协议用于层次结构评估，显著提升了图像分类、文本-图像检索和层次理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在欧几里得空间中操作，无法有效捕捉视觉和语言概念的固有层次结构。双曲几何因其指数级体积增长特性，为低失真嵌入层次结构提供了理论优势。然而，现有双曲VLM使用不稳定的蕴含损失：当父嵌入向原点收缩时，其蕴含锥会向半空间扩展，导致灾难性的锥崩溃，破坏预期的层次结构。此外，这些模型的层次评估方法不可靠，主要依赖检索和相关性指标，容易受分类学依赖和模糊负例影响。

Method: 提出了自适应蕴含损失配合范数正则化器，防止锥崩溃而无需启发式孔径裁剪。同时引入了基于角度的概率蕴含协议（PEP）用于评估层次理解，使用AUC-ROC和平均精度进行评分。该方法构成了ARGENT（自适应层次图像-文本表示）模型的基础。

Result: ARGENT在图像分类、文本到图像检索和提出的层次指标上分别比现有最佳双曲VLM提高了0.7、1.1和0.8个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: ARGENT通过自适应蕴含损失和范数正则化器有效解决了双曲VLM中的蕴含锥崩溃问题，同时提出的概率蕴含协议为层次结构评估提供了更可靠的度量标准，显著提升了双曲视觉语言模型的性能。

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP learn powerful semantic representations but operate in Euclidean space, which fails to capture the inherent hierarchical structure of visual and linguistic concepts. Hyperbolic geometry, with its exponential volume growth, offers a principled alternative for embedding such hierarchies with low distortion. However, existing hyperbolic VLMs use entailment losses that are unstable: as parent embeddings contract toward the origin, their entailment cones widen toward a half-space, causing catastrophic cone collapse that destroys the intended hierarchy. Additionally, hierarchical evaluation of these models remains unreliable, being largely retrieval-based and correlation-based metrics and prone to taxonomy dependence and ambiguous negatives. To address these limitations, we propose an adaptive entailment loss paired with a norm regularizer that prevents cone collapse without heuristic aperture clipping. We further introduce an angle-based probabilistic entailment protocol (PEP) for evaluating hierarchical understanding, scored with AUC-ROC and Average Precision. This paper introduces a stronger hyperbolic VLM baseline ARGENT, Adaptive hieRarchical imaGe-tExt represeNTation. ARGENT improves the SOTA hyperbolic VLM by 0.7, 1.1, and 0.8 absolute points on image classification, text-to-image retrieval, and proposed hierarchical metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [115] [Pose-Free Omnidirectional Gaussian Splatting for 360-Degree Videos with Consistent Depth Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23324)
*Chuanqing Zhuang,Xin Lu,Zehui Deng,Zhengda Lu,Yiqun Wang,Junqi Diao,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PFGS360：一种无需相机位姿的360度全景3D高斯泼溅方法，可从无位姿全景视频重建3D高斯表示，通过球面一致性位姿估计和深度内点感知的致密化实现高质量新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景3D高斯泼溅方法通常依赖缓慢的SfM提供相机位姿和稀疏点先验，限制了实际应用。需要开发无需位姿先验的方法直接从无位姿全景视频重建3D场景。

Method: 1. 球面一致性感知的位姿估计模块：利用高斯内部深度先验建立重建高斯与无位姿图像间的2D-3D对应关系，恢复相机位姿。2. 深度内点感知的致密化模块：利用一致的单目深度先验提取深度内点和高斯异常值，实现高效高斯致密化。

Result: 在真实世界和合成的360度视频上，PFGS360显著优于现有的无位姿和有位姿3DGS方法，实现了逼真的新视角合成效果。

Conclusion: PFGS360成功实现了无需相机位姿的全景3D高斯重建，通过创新的位姿估计和致密化策略，在质量和效率上都超越了现有方法，为全景3D场景表示提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Omnidirectional 3D Gaussian Splatting with panoramas is a key technique for 3D scene representation, and existing methods typically rely on slow SfM to provide camera poses and sparse points priors. In this work, we propose a pose-free omnidirectional 3DGS method, named PFGS360, that reconstructs 3D Gaussians from unposed omnidirectional videos. To achieve accurate camera pose estimation, we first construct a spherical consistency-aware pose estimation module, which recovers poses by establishing consistent 2D-3D correspondences between the reconstructed Gaussians and the unposed images using Gaussians' internal depth priors. Besides, to enhance the fidelity of novel view synthesis, we introduce a depth-inlier-aware densification module to extract depth inliers and Gaussian outliers with consistent monocular depth priors, enabling efficient Gaussian densification and achieving photorealistic novel view synthesis. The experiments show significant outperformance over existing pose-free and pose-aware 3DGS methods on both real-world and synthetic 360-degree videos. Code is available at https://github.com/zcq15/PFGS360.

</details>


### [116] [ViBe: Ultra-High-Resolution Video Synthesis Born from Pure Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23326)
*Yunfeng Wu,Hongying Cheng,Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Relay LoRA框架，通过两阶段纯图像适应将预训练视频扩散模型升级到超高分辨率视频生成，无需视频训练数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer视频扩散模型使用3D注意力机制，在空间和时间维度上具有二次复杂度，使得端到端训练超高分辨率视频极其昂贵。需要一种方法将预训练视频模型扩展到更高分辨率而不需要昂贵的视频数据训练。

Method: 1. Relay LoRA两阶段适应策略：第一阶段使用低分辨率图像适应视频模型到图像域以弥合模态差距；第二阶段使用高分辨率图像获得空间外推能力。2. 高频感知训练目标：通过专门的重建损失鼓励模型从退化潜在表示中恢复高频分量。3. 推理时只保留高分辨率适应以保持视频生成模态。

Result: 在VBench基准测试中超越先前在高质量视频上训练的最先进模型0.8分，能够生成具有丰富视觉细节的超高分辨率视频，且不需要任何视频训练数据。

Conclusion: 提出的纯图像适应框架成功将预训练视频扩散模型升级到超高分辨率视频生成，通过解耦模态对齐和空间外推学习目标，有效解决了图像-视频模态差距问题，实现了高效的高分辨率视频合成。

Abstract: Transformer-based video diffusion models rely on 3D attention over spatial and temporal tokens, which incurs quadratic time and memory complexity and makes end-to-end training for ultra-high-resolution videos prohibitively expensive. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose a pure image adaptation framework that upgrades a video Diffusion Transformer pre-trained at its native scale to synthesize higher-resolution videos. Unfortunately, naively fine-tuning with high-resolution images alone often introduces noticeable noise due to the image-video modality gap. To address this, we decouple the learning objective to separately handle modality alignment and spatial extrapolation. At the core of our approach is Relay LoRA, a two-stage adaptation strategy. In the first stage, the video diffusion model is adapted to the image domain using low-resolution images to bridge the modality gap. In the second stage, the model is further adapted with high-resolution images to acquire spatial extrapolation capability. During inference, only the high-resolution adaptation is retained to preserve the video generation modality while enabling high-resolution video synthesis. To enhance fine-grained detail synthesis, we further propose a High-Frequency-Awareness-Training-Objective, which explicitly encourages the model to recover high-frequency components from degraded latent representations via a dedicated reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces ultra-high-resolution videos with rich visual details without requiring any video training data, even outperforming previous state-of-the-art models trained on high-resolution videos by 0.8 on the VBench benchmark. Code will be available at https://github.com/WillWu111/ViBe.

</details>


### [117] [An Explainable AI-Driven Framework for Automated Brain Tumor Segmentation Using an Attention-Enhanced U-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23344)
*MD Rashidul Islam,Bakary Gibba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于U-Net架构的脑胶质瘤MRI分割方法，通过注意力门机制、定制化损失函数和可解释AI技术，在BraTS 2020数据集上实现了高精度分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑胶质瘤的准确分割对临床诊断、治疗规划和疾病监测至关重要。手动分割耗时且不可靠，而胶质瘤的高度恶性和异质性使得自动分割面临挑战，需要开发鲁棒的自动化技术。

Method: 采用U-Net模型架构，集成注意力门机制以聚焦图像关键区域。针对类别不平衡问题，结合使用Dice Loss、Categorical Dice Loss和标准分类交叉熵损失函数。引入基于Grad-CAM的可解释AI技术，通过高斯滤波生成平滑热力图以可视化注意力区域。

Result: 在BraTS 2020数据集上取得优异性能：准确率0.9919，Dice系数0.9901，平均IoU 0.9873，灵敏度0.9908，特异性0.9974。模型能有效区分背景/健康组织、坏死/非增强核心、水肿和增强肿瘤四个类别。

Conclusion: 研究表明，注意力机制、个性化损失函数和可解释AI技术的结合显著提高了复杂脑肿瘤结构的MRI分割精度，为临床应用提供了可靠且可解释的方法。

Abstract: Computer-aided segmentation of brain tumors from MRI data is of crucial significance to clinical decision-making in diagnosis, treatment planning, and follow-up disease monitoring. Gliomas, owing to their high malignancy and heterogeneity, represent a very challenging task for accurate and reliable segmentation into intra-tumoral sub-regions. Manual segmentation is typically time-consuming and not reliable, which justifies the need for robust automated techniques.This research resolves this problem by leveraging the BraTS 2020 dataset, where we have labeled MRI scans of glioma patients with four significant classes: background/healthy tissue, necrotic/non-enhancing core, edema, and enhancing tumor. In this work, we present a new segmentation technique based on a U-Net model augmented with executed attention gates to focus on the most significant regions of images. To counter class imbalance, we employ manually designed loss functions like Dice Loss and Categorical Dice Loss, in conjunction with standard categorical cross-entropy. Other evaluation metrics, like sensitivity and specificity, were used to measure discriminability of the model between tumor classes. Besides, we introduce Grad-CAM-based explainable AI to enable visualizing attention regions and improve model interpretability, together with a smooth heatmap generation technique through Gaussian filtering. Our approach achieved superior performance with accuracy of 0.9919, Dice coefficient of 0.9901, mean IoU of 0.9873, sensitivity of 0.9908, and specificity of 0.9974. This study demonstrates that the use of attention mechanisms, personalized loss functions, and explainable AI significantly improves highly complex tumor structure segmentation precision in MRI scans, providing a reliable and explainable method for clinical applications.

</details>


### [118] [Object Pose Transformer: Unifying Unseen Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23370)
*Weihang Li,Lorenzo Garattoni,Fabien Despinoy,Nassir Navab,Benjamin Busam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Object Pose Transformer (OPT) 是一个统一的单模型框架，通过任务分解同时解决类别级绝对位姿估计和未见对象相对位姿估计问题，无需推理时的语义标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分为两个独立的范式：类别级方法依赖预定义分类体系预测规范空间中的绝对位姿，而相对位姿方法估计跨视图变换但无法恢复单视图绝对位姿。需要一种统一框架来桥接这两个范式。

Method: 提出Object Pose Transformer (OPT)，一个前馈统一框架，通过任务分解联合预测深度、点图、相机参数和归一化对象坐标(NOCS)。利用对比性对象中心潜在嵌入进行规范化，无需推理时的语义标签；使用点图作为相机空间表示实现多视图相对几何推理；通过跨帧特征交互和共享对象嵌入，利用视图间的相对几何一致性改进绝对位姿估计。

Result: 在多个基准测试(NOCS, HouseCat6D, Omni6DPose, Toyota-Light)上的广泛实验表明，该模型在单一统一架构中，在绝对和相对位姿估计任务上都达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Object Pose Transformer 成功桥接了类别级绝对位姿估计和未见对象相对位姿估计两个范式，通过统一框架实现了两者的优势互补，同时支持相机无关性和可选深度输入。

Abstract: Learning model-free object pose estimation for unseen instances remains a fundamental challenge in 3D vision. Existing methods typically fall into two disjoint paradigms: category-level approaches predict absolute poses in a canonical space but rely on predefined taxonomies, while relative pose methods estimate cross-view transformations but cannot recover single-view absolute pose. In this work, we propose Object Pose Transformer (\ours{}), a unified feed-forward framework that bridges these paradigms through task factorization within a single model. \ours{} jointly predicts depth, point maps, camera parameters, and normalized object coordinates (NOCS) from RGB inputs, enabling both category-level absolute SA(3) pose and unseen-object relative SE(3) pose. Our approach leverages contrastive object-centric latent embeddings for canonicalization without requiring semantic labels at inference time, and uses point maps as a camera-space representation to enable multi-view relative geometric reasoning. Through cross-frame feature interaction and shared object embeddings, our model leverages relative geometric consistency across views to improve absolute pose estimation, reducing ambiguity in single-view predictions. Furthermore, \ours{} is camera-agnostic, learning camera intrinsics on-the-fly and supporting optional depth input for metric-scale recovery, while remaining fully functional in RGB-only settings. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks (NOCS, HouseCat6D, Omni6DPose, Toyota-Light) demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both absolute and relative pose estimation tasks within a single unified architecture.

</details>


### [119] [ABot-PhysWorld: Interactive World Foundation Model for Robotic Manipulation with Physics Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23376)
*Yuzhi Chen,Ronghan Chen,Dongjie Huo,Yandan Yang,Dekang Qi,Haoyun Liu,Tong Lin,Shuang Zeng,Junjin Xiao,Xinyuan Chang,Feng Xiong,Xing Wei,Zhiheng Ma,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABot-PhysWorld是一个14B参数的扩散Transformer模型，通过物理感知标注的数据集和DPO后训练框架，生成视觉逼真、物理合理且动作可控的视频，在物理合理性和轨迹一致性上超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的世界模型在生成物理操作时经常产生物理上不合理的结果（如物体穿透、反重力运动），这是因为它们基于通用视觉数据训练，且使用忽略物理规律的似然目标函数。

Method: 1. 基于包含300万操作片段且带有物理感知标注的精选数据集；2. 采用新颖的基于DPO的后训练框架，使用解耦判别器抑制非物理行为同时保持视觉质量；3. 使用并行上下文块实现精确的空间动作注入以实现跨具身控制。

Result: 1. 在PBench和EZSbench上达到新的最先进性能；2. 在物理合理性和轨迹一致性方面超越Veo 3.1和Sora v2 Pro；3. 引入EZSbench作为首个训练独立的具身零样本基准，结合真实和合成的未见机器人-任务-场景组合。

Conclusion: ABot-PhysWorld通过物理感知训练和解耦判别器框架，显著提升了视频世界模型的物理合理性和动作可控性，为具身模拟和规划提供了更可靠的解决方案，并将发布EZSbench以促进标准化评估。

Abstract: Video-based world models offer a powerful paradigm for embodied simulation and planning, yet state-of-the-art models often generate physically implausible manipulations - such as object penetration and anti-gravity motion - due to training on generic visual data and likelihood-based objectives that ignore physical laws. We present ABot-PhysWorld, a 14B Diffusion Transformer model that generates visually realistic, physically plausible, and action-controllable videos. Built on a curated dataset of three million manipulation clips with physics-aware annotation, it uses a novel DPO-based post-training framework with decoupled discriminators to suppress unphysical behaviors while preserving visual quality. A parallel context block enables precise spatial action injection for cross-embodiment control. To better evaluate generalization, we introduce EZSbench, the first training-independent embodied zero-shot benchmark combining real and synthetic unseen robot-task-scene combinations. It employs a decoupled protocol to separately assess physical realism and action alignment. ABot-PhysWorld achieves new state-of-the-art performance on PBench and EZSbench, surpassing Veo 3.1 and Sora v2 Pro in physical plausibility and trajectory consistency. We will release EZSbench to promote standardized evaluation in embodied video generation.

</details>


### [120] [FG-Portrait: 3D Flow Guided Editable Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23381)
*Yating Xu,Yunqi Miao,Evangelos Ververas,Jiankang Deng,Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D Flow Diffusion方法，通过3D流对应关系改进肖像动画中的运动迁移，解决现有方法在源图像与驱动图像对应关系捕捉不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散模型的肖像动画方法仅依赖驱动运动作为条件，无法有效捕捉源图像与驱动图像之间的对应关系，导致运动迁移效果不理想。虽然光流估计提供了替代方案，但从2D输入预测密集对应关系是病态问题，往往产生不准确的动画效果。

Method: 1. 引入3D流：从参数化3D头部模型直接计算的学习无关、几何驱动的运动对应关系；2. 3D流编码：将3D先验整合到扩散模型中，为每个目标像素查询潜在的3D流以指示其回源位置的位移；3. 深度引导采样：精确定位每个像素对应的3D点，确保3D流与2D运动变化对齐。

Result: 该方法在一致的驱动运动迁移和忠实的源身份保持方面表现出优越性，支持用户指定的面部表情和头部姿态编辑，实现了高质量的肖像动画。

Conclusion: 通过引入3D流对应关系和相应的编码与采样机制，有效解决了肖像动画中运动迁移的关键挑战，显著提升了动画质量和控制能力。

Abstract: Motion transfer from the driving to the source portrait remains a key challenge in the portrait animation. Current diffusion-based approaches condition only on the driving motion, which fails to capture source-to-driving correspondences and consequently yields suboptimal motion transfer. Although flow estimation provides an alternative, predicting dense correspondences from 2D input is ill-posed and often yields inaccurate animation. We address this problem by introducing 3D flows, a learning-free and geometry-driven motion correspondence directly computed from parametric 3D head models. To integrate this 3D prior into diffusion model, we introduce 3D flow encoding to query potential 3D flows for each target pixel to indicate its displacement back to the source location. To obtain 3D flows aligned with 2D motion changes, we further propose depth-guided sampling to accurately locate the corresponding 3D points for each pixel. Beyond high-fidelity portrait animation, our model further supports user-specified editing of facial expression and head pose. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method on consistent driving motion transfer as well as faithful source identity preservation.

</details>


### [121] [SIMART: Decomposing Monolithic Meshes into Sim-ready Articulated Assets via MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23386)
*Chuanrui Zhang,Minghan Qin,Yuang Wang,Baifeng Xie,Hang Li,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIMART是一个统一的MLLM框架，通过稀疏3D VQ-VAE减少70%的token数量，实现高质量可交互的关节化3D资产生成，支持物理机器人仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D生成主要关注静态网格，缺乏"仿真就绪"的可交互对象。现有关节化对象创建方法依赖多阶段流水线，误差在解耦模块间累积；而统一MLLM方法因密集体素3D tokenization产生长token序列和高内存开销，限制了复杂关节化对象的可扩展性。

Method: 提出SIMART统一MLLM框架，联合执行部件级分解和运动学预测。通过引入稀疏3D VQ-VAE，相比密集体素token减少70%的token数量，实现高保真多部件装配。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility和野外AIGC数据集上达到最先进性能，并支持基于物理的机器人仿真。

Conclusion: SIMART通过稀疏3D tokenization解决了统一MLLM框架在复杂关节化对象生成中的可扩展性问题，为具身AI和物理仿真提供了高质量的"仿真就绪"交互式3D资产。

Abstract: High-quality articulated 3D assets are indispensable for embodied AI and physical simulation, yet 3D generation still focuses on static meshes, leaving a gap in "sim-ready" interactive objects. Most recent articulated object creation methods rely on multi-stage pipelines that accumulate errors across decoupled modules. Alternatively, unified MLLMs offer a single-stage path to joint static asset understanding and sim-ready asset generation. However dense voxel-based 3D tokenization yields long 3D token sequences and high memory overhead, limiting scalability to complex articulated objects. To address this, we propose SIMART, a unified MLLM framework that jointly performs part-level decomposition and kinematic prediction. By introducing a Sparse 3D VQ-VAE, SIMART reduces token counts by 70% vs. dense voxel tokens, enabling high-fidelity multi-part assemblies. SIMART achieves state-of-the-art performance on PartNet-Mobility and in-the-wild AIGC datasets, and enables physics-based robotic simulation.

</details>


### [122] [Harnessing Lightweight Transformer with Contextual Synergic Enhancement for Efficient 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23390)
*Xinyu Liu,Zhen Chen,Wuyang Li,Chenxin Li,Yixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Light-UNETR：一种轻量级Transformer架构，通过LIDR模块和CGLU实现模型效率，通过CSE学习策略提升数据效率，在3D医学图像分割中显著减少计算量和参数的同时保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在3D医学图像分割中面临两个主要挑战：1）计算需求高，模型参数量大；2）需要大量标注数据。这些限制阻碍了Transformer在实际临床环境中的应用，特别是在标注数据稀缺的场景下。

Method: 提出Light-UNETR框架，包含两个核心创新：1）模型效率方面：设计轻量级维度缩减注意力模块（LIDR），通过多分支注意力同时捕获全局和局部特征，同时减少空间和通道维度；引入紧凑门控线性单元（CGLU），以最少的参数选择性控制通道交互。2）数据效率方面：提出上下文协同增强学习策略（CSE），包括注意力引导替换（利用外部上下文信息支持未标注数据学习）和空间掩码一致性（利用内部上下文信息增强未标注数据的空间上下文推理）。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现出优越的性能和效率。以左心房分割数据集为例，仅使用10%标注数据时，Jaccard指标比BCP方法提升1.43%，同时FLOPs减少90.8%，参数量减少85.8%。

Conclusion: Light-UNETR成功解决了Transformer在3D医学图像分割中的计算和数据效率问题，通过创新的轻量化设计和半监督学习策略，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低计算资源需求，为Transformer在资源受限环境中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformers have shown remarkable performance in 3D medical image segmentation, but their high computational requirements and need for large amounts of labeled data limit their applicability. To address these challenges, we consider two crucial aspects: model efficiency and data efficiency. Specifically, we propose Light-UNETR, a lightweight transformer designed to achieve model efficiency. Light-UNETR features a Lightweight Dimension Reductive Attention (LIDR) module, which reduces spatial and channel dimensions while capturing both global and local features via multi-branch attention. Additionally, we introduce a Compact Gated Linear Unit (CGLU) to selectively control channel interaction with minimal parameters. Furthermore, we introduce a Contextual Synergic Enhancement (CSE) learning strategy, which aims to boost the data efficiency of Transformers. It first leverages the extrinsic contextual information to support the learning of unlabeled data with Attention-Guided Replacement, then applies Spatial Masking Consistency that utilizes intrinsic contextual information to enhance the spatial context reasoning for unlabeled data. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach in both performance and efficiency. For example, with only 10% labeled data on the Left Atrial Segmentation dataset, our method surpasses BCP by 1.43% Jaccard while drastically reducing the FLOPs by 90.8% and parameters by 85.8%. Code is released at https://github.com/CUHK-AIM-Group/Light-UNETR.

</details>


### [123] [I3DM: Implicit 3D-aware Memory Retrieval and Injection for Consistent Video Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23413)
*Jia Li,Han Yan,Yihang Chen,Siqi Li,Xibin Song,Yifu Wang,Jianfei Cai,Tien-Tsin Wong,Pan Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: I3DM提出了一种隐式3D感知记忆机制，用于解决视频生成中长期场景一致性问题，特别是在重新访问先前探索区域时，无需显式3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法在重新访问先前探索区域时难以保持长期场景一致性。现有解决方案要么依赖显式3D几何构建（存在误差累积和尺度模糊问题），要么使用简单的相机视场检索（在复杂遮挡场景下通常失败）。

Method: 提出I3DM：1）3D感知记忆检索策略，利用预训练前馈新视角合成模型的中间特征来评估视角相关性，实现复杂遮挡下的鲁棒检索；2）3D对齐记忆注入模块，隐式地将历史内容扭曲到目标视角，并自适应地基于可靠扭曲区域调节生成过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在重新访问一致性、生成保真度和相机控制精度方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: I3DM通过隐式3D感知记忆机制，无需显式3D重建即可实现长期场景一致性视频生成，在复杂遮挡场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in video generation, maintaining long-term scene consistency upon revisiting previously explored areas remains challenging. Existing solutions rely either on explicitly constructing 3D geometry, which suffers from error accumulation and scale ambiguity, or on naive camera Field-of-View (FoV) retrieval, which typically fails under complex occlusions. To overcome these limitations, we propose I3DM, a novel implicit 3D-aware memory mechanism for consistent video scene generation that bypasses explicit 3D reconstruction. At the core of our approach is a 3D-aware memory retrieval strategy, which leverages the intermediate features of a pre-trained Feed-Forward Novel View Synthesis (FF-NVS) model to score view relevance, enabling robust retrieval even in highly occluded scenarios. Furthermore, to fully utilize the retrieved historical frames, we introduce a 3D-aligned memory injection module. This module implicitly warps historical content to the target view and adaptively conditions the generation on reliable warping regions, leading to improved revisit consistency and accurate camera control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving superior revisit consistency, generation fidelity, and camera control precision.

</details>


### [124] [SIGMA: A Physics-Based Benchmark for Gas Chimney Understanding in Seismic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23439)
*Bao Truong,Quang Nguyen,Baoru Huang,Jinpei Han,Van Nguyen,Ngan Le,Minh-Tan Pham,Doan Huy Hien,Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA数据集，用于地震图像中气烟囱的检测与增强，包含像素级掩码和退化-真值图像对，基于物理方法生成，覆盖多种地质条件和采集条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震图像中的气烟囱检测对油气勘探和钻井安全至关重要，但传统物理方法计算成本高且对模型误差敏感，深度学习方法缺乏标注数据集。

Method: 创建SIGMA数据集，包含像素级气烟囱掩码用于检测，以及成对的退化图像和地面真值图像用于增强。采用基于物理的方法生成数据，覆盖广泛的地质设置和数据采集条件。

Result: SIGMA数据集为气烟囱解释提供了具有挑战性的基准测试，并有益于一般地震图像理解。综合实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SIGMA数据集解决了气烟囱检测中标注数据缺乏的问题，为基于深度学习的检测和增强方法提供了高质量的基准数据集。

Abstract: Seismic images reconstruct subsurface reflectivity from field recordings, guiding exploration and reservoir monitoring. Gas chimneys are vertical anomalies caused by subsurface fluid migration. Understanding these phenomena is crucial for assessing hydrocarbon potential and avoiding drilling hazards. However, accurate detection is challenging due to strong seismic attenuation and scattering. Traditional physics-based methods are computationally expensive and sensitive to model errors, while deep learning offers efficient alternatives, yet lacks labeled datasets. In this work, we introduce \textbf{SIGMA}, a new physics-based dataset for gas chimney understanding in seismic images, featuring (i) pixel-level gas-chimney mask for detection and (ii) paired degraded and ground-truth image for enhancement. We employed physics-based methods that cover a wide range of geological settings and data acquisition conditions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that SIGMA serves as a challenging benchmark for gas chimney interpretation and benefits general seismic understanding.

</details>


### [125] [3DCity-LLM: Empowering Multi-modality Large Language Models for 3D City-scale Perception and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23447)
*Yiping Chen,Jinpeng Li,Wenyu Ke,Yang Luo,Jie Ouyang,Zhongjie He,Li Liu,Hongchao Fan,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DCity-LLM：针对3D城市尺度环境的统一视觉语言感知与理解框架，通过粗到细特征编码和多任务数据集实现城市智能推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型主要关注物体中心或室内场景，难以扩展到3D城市尺度环境，需要专门框架处理城市级空间推理和感知任务

Method: 提出统一框架3DCity-LLM，采用粗到细特征编码策略，包含目标物体、物体间关系和全局场景三个并行分支；构建3DCity-LLM-1.2M数据集，包含120万高质量样本，涵盖7个任务类别；使用基于文本相似度和LLM语义评估的多维度评估协议

Result: 在两个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，3DCity-LLM显著优于现有最先进方法，为空间推理和城市智能提供了有前景的方向

Conclusion: 3DCity-LLM成功填补了多模态大语言模型在3D城市尺度环境中的空白，通过统一框架、大规模高质量数据集和全面评估协议，推动了城市智能和空间推理的发展

Abstract: While multi-modality large language models excel in object-centric or indoor scenarios, scaling them to 3D city-scale environments remains a formidable challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose 3DCity-LLM, a unified framework designed for 3D city-scale vision-language perception and understanding. 3DCity-LLM employs a coarse-to-fine feature encoding strategy comprising three parallel branches for target object, inter-object relationship, and global scene. To facilitate large-scale training, we introduce 3DCity-LLM-1.2M dataset that comprises approximately 1.2 million high-quality samples across seven representative task categories, ranging from fine-grained object analysis to multi-faceted scene planning. This strictly quality-controlled dataset integrates explicit 3D numerical information and diverse user-oriented simulations, enriching the question-answering diversity and realism of urban scenarios. Furthermore, we apply a multi-dimensional protocol based on text-similarity metrics and LLM-based semantic assessment to ensure faithful and comprehensive evaluations for all methods. Extensive experiments on two benchmarks demonstrate that 3DCity-LLM significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, offering a promising and meaningful direction for advancing spatial reasoning and urban intelligence. The source code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SYSU-3DSTAILab/3D-City-LLM.

</details>


### [126] [DetPO: In-Context Learning with Multi-Modal LLMs for Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23455)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Neehar Peri,Matvei Popov,Shruti Jain,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DetPO是一种无需梯度的测试时优化方法，通过最大化少样本视觉训练示例的检测准确率并校准预测置信度，优化纯文本提示以提升多模态大语言模型在少样本目标检测中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在目标检测任务中面临以下问题：1) 对分布外类别、任务和成像模态泛化能力有限；2) 上下文提示（包含少样本视觉示例和丰富文本描述）的检测准确率反而低于仅使用类别名称的提示；3) 前沿模型通常只能通过API访问，而开源模型在消费级硬件上微调成本过高。

Method: 提出检测提示优化（DetPO），一种无需梯度的测试时优化方法。该方法通过最大化少样本视觉训练示例的检测准确率并校准预测置信度，在测试时优化纯文本提示。DetPO采用黑盒优化策略，不需要访问模型内部梯度，适用于API访问的模型。

Result: 在Roboflow20-VL和LVIS数据集上的实验表明，DetPO在通用多模态大语言模型上带来了一致的性能提升，比先前黑盒方法高出最多9.7%。该方法有效提升了少样本目标检测的准确率。

Conclusion: DetPO提供了一种实用且有效的黑盒提示优化方法，能够显著提升多模态大语言模型在少样本目标检测任务中的性能，特别是在无法进行模型微调或访问内部梯度的情况下。

Abstract: Multi-Modal LLMs (MLLMs) demonstrate strong visual grounding capabilities on popular object detection benchmarks like OdinW-13 and RefCOCO. However, state-of-the-art models still struggle to generalize to out-of-distribution classes, tasks and imaging modalities not typically found in their pre-training. While in-context prompting is a common strategy to improve performance across diverse tasks, we find that it often yields lower detection accuracy than prompting with class names alone. This suggests that current MLLMs cannot yet effectively leverage few-shot visual examples and rich textual descriptions for object detection. Since frontier MLLMs are typically only accessible via APIs, and state-of-the-art open-weights models are prohibitively expensive to fine-tune on consumer-grade hardware, we instead explore black-box prompt optimization for few-shot object detection. To this end, we propose Detection Prompt Optimization (DetPO), a gradient-free test-time optimization approach that refines text-only prompts by maximizing detection accuracy on few-shot visual training examples while calibrating prediction confidence. Our proposed approach yields consistent improvements across generalist MLLMs on Roboflow20-VL and LVIS, outperforming prior black-box approaches by up to 9.7%. Our code is available at https://github.com/ggare-cmu/DetPO

</details>


### [127] [RealMaster: Lifting Rendered Scenes into Photorealistic Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23462)
*Dana Cohen-Bar,Ido Sobol,Raphael Bensadoun,Shelly Sheynin,Oran Gafni,Or Patashnik,Daniel Cohen-Or,Amit Zohar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealMaster利用视频扩散模型将3D引擎渲染的视频提升为逼真视频，同时保持与原始3D控制的几何、动态和身份一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型缺乏精确控制且无法保证3D一致性，而3D引擎虽然提供精确控制但输出不够逼真，存在"恐怖谷"效应。需要同时满足结构精度（保持输入几何和动态）和全局语义转换（实现材质、光照和纹理的逼真化）

Method: 通过锚点传播策略生成配对数据集：增强首尾帧的逼真度，利用几何条件线索传播到中间帧；然后在这些配对视频上训练IC-LoRA，将高质量输出蒸馏到模型中，使其能够处理中途出现的对象和角色，且无需锚点帧

Result: 在复杂的GTA-V序列上评估，RealMaster显著优于现有视频编辑基线，在保持原始3D控制指定的几何、动态和身份的同时，提高了逼真度

Conclusion: RealMaster成功弥合了模拟到现实的差距，实现了结构精度和全局语义转换的平衡，为3D引擎渲染提供了逼真化解决方案

Abstract: State-of-the-art video generation models produce remarkable photorealism, but they lack the precise control required to align generated content with specific scene requirements. Furthermore, without an underlying explicit geometry, these models cannot guarantee 3D consistency. Conversely, 3D engines offer granular control over every scene element and provide native 3D consistency by design, yet their output often remains trapped in the "uncanny valley". Bridging this sim-to-real gap requires both structural precision, where the output must exactly preserve the geometry and dynamics of the input, and global semantic transformation, where materials, lighting, and textures must be holistically transformed to achieve photorealism. We present RealMaster, a method that leverages video diffusion models to lift rendered video into photorealistic video while maintaining full alignment with the output of the 3D engine. To train this model, we generate a paired dataset via an anchor-based propagation strategy, where the first and last frames are enhanced for realism and propagated across the intermediate frames using geometric conditioning cues. We then train an IC-LoRA on these paired videos to distill the high-quality outputs of the pipeline into a model that generalizes beyond the pipeline's constraints, handling objects and characters that appear mid-sequence and enabling inference without requiring anchor frames. Evaluated on complex GTA-V sequences, RealMaster significantly outperforms existing video editing baselines, improving photorealism while preserving the geometry, dynamics, and identity specified by the original 3D control.

</details>


### [128] [InverFill: One-Step Inversion for Enhanced Few-Step Diffusion Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23463)
*Duc Vu,Kien Nguyen,Trong-Tung Nguyen,Ngan Nguyen,Phong Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen,Cuong Pham,Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InverFill：一种针对图像修复的单步反转方法，通过将输入掩码图像的语义信息注入初始噪声，实现高质量少步修复，无需训练专门的修复模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像修复中需要大量采样步骤，限制了实际应用；而少步文本到图像模型直接应用于修复时，由于随机高斯噪声初始化导致语义错位和伪影，背景与修复区域协调性差

Method: 提出InverFill单步反转方法：1）从输入掩码图像中提取语义信息注入初始噪声；2）在混合采样流程中利用少步文本到图像模型；3）使用语义对齐的噪声作为输入，无需真实图像监督，仅增加最小推理开销

Result: InverFill显著提升基线少步模型性能，改善图像质量和文本一致性，在低NFE（函数评估次数）下匹配专用修复模型，大幅优于原始混合采样方法

Conclusion: InverFill通过语义对齐的噪声初始化解决了少步修复中的协调问题，无需昂贵重训练或繁重迭代优化，为高效高质量图像修复提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based models achieve photorealism in image inpainting but require many sampling steps, limiting practical use. Few-step text-to-image models offer faster generation, but naively applying them to inpainting yields poor harmonization and artifacts between the background and inpainted region. We trace this cause to random Gaussian noise initialization, which under low function evaluations causes semantic misalignment and reduced fidelity. To overcome this, we propose InverFill, a one-step inversion method tailored for inpainting that injects semantic information from the input masked image into the initial noise, enabling high-fidelity few-step inpainting. Instead of training inpainting models, InverFill leverages few-step text-to-image models in a blended sampling pipeline with semantically aligned noise as input, significantly improving vanilla blended sampling and even matching specialized inpainting models at low NFEs. Moreover, InverFill does not require real-image supervision and only adds minimal inference overhead. Extensive experiments show that InverFill consistently boosts baseline few-step models, improving image quality and text coherence without costly retraining or heavy iterative optimization.

</details>


### [129] [UniFunc3D: Unified Active Spatial-Temporal Grounding for 3D Functionality Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23478)
*Jiaying Lin,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniFunc3D：一种用于3D场景功能分割的统一免训练框架，通过主动时空定位和粗到细策略，在SceneFun3D基准上实现SOTA性能，相对提升59.9% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景功能分割方法依赖碎片化流程，在初始任务解析时存在视觉盲区，受限于单尺度、被动和启发式的帧选择策略，需要更统一的主动推理框架。

Method: 将多模态大语言模型作为主动观察者，通过单次前向传播整合语义、时间和空间推理，采用主动时空定位和粗到细策略，自适应选择正确视频帧并聚焦高细节交互部分。

Result: 在SceneFun3D基准上实现最先进性能，大幅超越免训练和基于训练的方法，相对提升59.9% mIoU，无需任何任务特定训练。

Conclusion: UniFunc3D通过统一框架解决了现有方法的视觉盲区问题，证明了主动观察和多模态大语言模型在3D场景功能分割中的有效性，为细粒度交互元素定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: Functionality segmentation in 3D scenes requires an agent to ground implicit natural-language instructions into precise masks of fine-grained interactive elements. Existing methods rely on fragmented pipelines that suffer from visual blindness during initial task parsing. We observe that these methods are limited by single-scale, passive and heuristic frame selection. We present UniFunc3D, a unified and training-free framework that treats the multimodal large language model as an active observer. By consolidating semantic, temporal, and spatial reasoning into a single forward pass, UniFunc3D performs joint reasoning to ground task decomposition in direct visual evidence. Our approach introduces active spatial-temporal grounding with a coarse-to-fine strategy. This allows the model to select correct video frames adaptively and focus on high-detail interactive parts while preserving the global context necessary for disambiguation. On SceneFun3D, UniFunc3D achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing both training-free and training-based methods by a large margin with a relative 59.9\% mIoU improvement, without any task-specific training. Code will be released on our project page: https://jiaying.link/unifunc3d.

</details>


### [130] [TETO: Tracking Events with Teacher Observation for Motion Estimation and Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23487)
*Jini Yang,Eunbeen Hong,Soowon Son,Hyunkoo Lee,Sunghwan Hong,Sunok Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TETO：一种教师-学生框架，通过从预训练RGB跟踪器进行知识蒸馏，仅使用约25分钟未标注真实世界事件数据学习运动估计，实现点轨迹和稠密光流预测，并用于视频帧插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于事件的运动估计方法依赖大规模合成数据，存在显著的模拟到真实差距。需要一种能够从少量真实世界事件数据中学习运动估计的方法。

Method: 提出TETO教师-学生框架：1）使用预训练RGB跟踪器作为教师模型进行知识蒸馏；2）设计运动感知数据筛选和查询采样策略，从主导的自我运动中解耦物体运动；3）联合预测点轨迹和稠密光流；4）将运动估计作为显式运动先验，用于条件化预训练视频扩散变换器进行帧插值。

Result: 在EVIMO2上实现最先进的点跟踪性能，在DSEC上实现最先进的光流性能，训练数据量减少数个数量级。准确的运动估计直接转化为BS-ERGB和HQ-EVFI上卓越的帧插值质量。

Conclusion: TETO通过知识蒸馏从少量真实世界事件数据中学习运动估计，解决了模拟到真实差距问题，实现了高质量的点跟踪、光流预测和帧插值，为事件相机运动估计提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Event cameras capture per-pixel brightness changes with microsecond resolution, offering continuous motion information lost between RGB frames. However, existing event-based motion estimators depend on large-scale synthetic data that often suffers from a significant sim-to-real gap. We propose TETO (Tracking Events with Teacher Observation), a teacher-student framework that learns event motion estimation from only $\sim$25 minutes of unannotated real-world recordings through knowledge distillation from a pretrained RGB tracker. Our motion-aware data curation and query sampling strategy maximizes learning from limited data by disentangling object motion from dominant ego-motion. The resulting estimator jointly predicts point trajectories and dense optical flow, which we leverage as explicit motion priors to condition a pretrained video diffusion transformer for frame interpolation. We achieve state-of-the-art point tracking on EVIMO2 and optical flow on DSEC using orders of magnitude less training data, and demonstrate that accurate motion estimation translates directly to superior frame interpolation quality on BS-ERGB and HQ-EVFI.

</details>


### [131] [One View Is Enough! Monocular Training for In-the-Wild Novel View Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23488)
*Adrien Ramanana Rahary,Nicolas Dufour,Patrick Perez,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVIE：仅使用单张未配对互联网图像进行单目新视角合成的训练方法，无需多视角监督，推理时无需几何信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目新视角合成需要多视角图像对进行监督，限制了训练数据的规模和多样性。作者认为单张图像就足够了，希望利用互联网上大量未配对的单视角图像进行训练。

Method: 1. 使用单目深度估计器作为训练时的几何支架：将源图像提升到3D空间，应用采样的相机变换，投影得到伪目标视角。2. 为处理遮挡问题，引入掩码训练公式，将几何、感知和纹理损失限制在有效区域。3. 在30百万张未筛选的互联网图像上训练。4. 推理时完全无需几何信息，不需要深度估计器或3D表示。

Result: 1. 在零样本设置下优于先前方法。2. 推理速度比第二好的基线快600倍。3. 完全在野外图像上训练，无需多视角监督。

Conclusion: OVIE证明了仅使用单张未配对图像就能训练高质量的新视角合成模型，打破了传统多视角监督的限制，实现了高效、高质量的零样本新视角生成。

Abstract: Monocular novel-view synthesis has long required multi-view image pairs for supervision, limiting training data scale and diversity. We argue it is not necessary: one view is enough. We present OVIE, trained entirely on unpaired internet images. We leverage a monocular depth estimator as a geometric scaffold at training time: we lift a source image into 3D, apply a sampled camera transformation, and project to obtain a pseudo-target view. To handle disocclusions, we introduce a masked training formulation that restricts geometric, perceptual, and textural losses to valid regions, enabling training on 30 million uncurated images. At inference, OVIE is geometry-free, requiring no depth estimator or 3D representation. Trained exclusively on in-the-wild images, OVIE outperforms prior methods in a zero-shot setting, while being 600x faster than the second-best baseline. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/AdrienRR/ovie.

</details>


### [132] [Foveated Diffusion: Efficient Spatially Adaptive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23491)
*Brian Chao,Lior Yariv,Howard Xiao,Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于人眼注视位置的混合分辨率生成方法，利用人眼视觉的偏心度依赖特性，在注视区域使用高分辨率、外围区域使用低分辨率，显著减少生成所需的token数量和时间，同时保持感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配模型在创意内容生成方面展现出强大能力，但随着分辨率、帧率和上下文长度的增加，计算复杂度呈二次增长，生成效率成为挑战。本文旨在利用已知或可估计的用户注视位置，基于人眼视觉的偏心度依赖特性优化生成效率。

Method: 1. 使用掩模建模中央凹分辨率，非均匀分配token密度（注视区域高密度，外围区域低密度）；2. 在混合分辨率token设置下生成图像或视频；3. 开发从高分辨率数据直接构建混合分辨率token的原理性机制；4. 通过后训练现有基础模型实现中央凹扩散模型，保持跨分辨率的内容一致性。

Result: 通过广泛分析和精心设计的用户研究验证，该方法在感知上与全分辨率生成无法区分，同时显著减少token数量和生成时间，证明中央凹化作为高效生成的实际可扩展轴的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于人眼注视位置的混合分辨率生成方法能够在不牺牲感知质量的前提下大幅提升生成效率，为扩散模型的高效生成提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，特别适用于已知用户注视位置的应用场景。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching models have unlocked unprecedented capabilities for creative content creation, such as interactive image and streaming video generation. The growing demand for higher resolutions, frame rates, and context lengths, however, makes efficient generation increasingly challenging, as computational complexity grows quadratically with the number of generated tokens. Our work seeks to optimize the efficiency of the generation process in settings where the user's gaze location is known or can be estimated, for example, by using eye tracking. In these settings, we leverage the eccentricity-dependent acuity of human vision: while a user perceives very high-resolution visual information in a small region around their gaze location (the foveal region), the ability to resolve detail quickly degrades in the periphery of the visual field. Our approach starts with a mask modeling the foveated resolution to allocate tokens non-uniformly, assigning higher token density to foveal regions and lower density to peripheral regions. An image or video is generated in a mixed-resolution token setting, yielding results perceptually indistinguishable from full-resolution generation, while drastically reducing the token count and generation time. To this end, we develop a principled mechanism for constructing mixed-resolution tokens directly from high-resolution data, allowing a foveated diffusion model to be post-trained from an existing base model while maintaining content consistency across resolutions. We validate our approach through extensive analysis and a carefully designed user study, demonstrating the efficacy of foveation as a practical and scalable axis for efficient generation.

</details>


### [133] [WildWorld: A Large-Scale Dataset for Dynamic World Modeling with Actions and Explicit State toward Generative ARPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23497)
*Zhen Li,Zian Meng,Shuwei Shi,Wenshuo Peng,Yuwei Wu,Bo Zheng,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildWorld是一个大规模动作条件世界建模数据集，从AAA游戏《怪物猎人：荒野》自动收集，包含1.08亿帧、450多种动作，并带有角色骨骼、世界状态、相机位姿和深度图等逐帧标注，用于评估动作条件世界模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型数据集存在两个主要问题：1) 缺乏多样化和语义丰富的动作空间；2) 动作直接与视觉观察绑定而非通过潜在状态中介，导致动作与像素级变化纠缠，难以学习结构化世界动态和保持长期一致性。

Method: 从AAA动作角色扮演游戏《怪物猎人：荒野》自动收集数据，构建包含1.08亿帧的大规模数据集，标注450多种动作（移动、攻击、技能施放等），并提供逐帧的角色骨骼、世界状态、相机位姿和深度图同步标注。进一步开发WildBench评估基准，包含动作跟随和状态对齐两个任务。

Result: 实验揭示了在建模语义丰富动作和保持长期状态一致性方面的持续挑战，突显了状态感知视频生成的需求。数据集规模为108M帧，包含450+动作，提供了全面的状态标注。

Conclusion: WildWorld填补了现有世界建模数据集的空白，通过提供大规模、多样动作空间和显式状态标注的数据，为动作条件世界模型研究提供了重要资源。实验结果强调了状态感知视频生成的重要性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Dynamical systems theory and reinforcement learning view world evolution as latent-state dynamics driven by actions, with visual observations providing partial information about the state. Recent video world models attempt to learn this action-conditioned dynamics from data. However, existing datasets rarely match the requirement: they typically lack diverse and semantically meaningful action spaces, and actions are directly tied to visual observations rather than mediated by underlying states. As a result, actions are often entangled with pixel-level changes, making it difficult for models to learn structured world dynamics and maintain consistent evolution over long horizons. In this paper, we propose WildWorld, a large-scale action-conditioned world modeling dataset with explicit state annotations, automatically collected from a photorealistic AAA action role-playing game (Monster Hunter: Wilds). WildWorld contains over 108 million frames and features more than 450 actions, including movement, attacks, and skill casting, together with synchronized per-frame annotations of character skeletons, world states, camera poses, and depth maps. We further derive WildBench to evaluate models through Action Following and State Alignment. Extensive experiments reveal persistent challenges in modeling semantically rich actions and maintaining long-horizon state consistency, highlighting the need for state-aware video generation. The project page is https://shandaai.github.io/wildworld-project/.

</details>


### [134] [DA-Flow: Degradation-Aware Optical Flow Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23499)
*Jaewon Min,Jaeeun Lee,Yeji Choi,Paul Hyunbin Cho,Jin Hyeon Kim,Tae-Young Lee,Jongsik Ahn,Hwayeong Lee,Seonghyun Park,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DA-Flow方法，通过融合扩散模型特征与卷积特征，在严重退化条件下实现鲁棒的光流估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光流模型在高质量数据上训练，但在面对真实世界退化（如模糊、噪声、压缩伪影）时性能严重下降，需要开发能够处理退化视频的鲁棒光流估计方法

Method: 提出Degradation-Aware Optical Flow任务，利用图像恢复扩散模型的中间表示（具有退化感知能力但缺乏时间感知），通过全时空注意力机制扩展到相邻帧，构建DA-Flow混合架构，融合扩散特征与卷积特征，采用迭代细化框架

Result: DA-Flow在多个基准测试中，在严重退化条件下显著优于现有光流方法，表现出零样本对应能力

Conclusion: 扩散模型的中间表示具有内在的退化感知能力，通过时空注意力扩展可提供零样本对应特征，与卷积特征融合的混合架构能有效处理真实世界退化视频的光流估计问题

Abstract: Optical flow models trained on high-quality data often degrade severely when confronted with real-world corruptions such as blur, noise, and compression artifacts. To overcome this limitation, we formulate Degradation-Aware Optical Flow, a new task targeting accurate dense correspondence estimation from real-world corrupted videos. Our key insight is that the intermediate representations of image restoration diffusion models are inherently corruption-aware but lack temporal awareness. To address this limitation, we lift the model to attend across adjacent frames via full spatio-temporal attention, and empirically demonstrate that the resulting features exhibit zero-shot correspondence capabilities. Based on this finding, we present DA-Flow, a hybrid architecture that fuses these diffusion features with convolutional features within an iterative refinement framework. DA-Flow substantially outperforms existing optical flow methods under severe degradation across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [UniGRPO: Unified Policy Optimization for Reasoning-Driven Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23500)
*Jie Liu,Zilyu Ye,Linxiao Yuan,Shenhan Zhu,Yu Gao,Jie Wu,Kunchang Li,Xionghui Wang,Xiaonan Nie,Weilin Huang,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一强化学习框架UniGRPO，用于优化交错生成任务中的文本推理和图像生成策略，通过改进FlowGRPO实现可扩展的多轮交错生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着交错生成统一模型成为有前景的范式，社区逐渐采用自回归建模处理文本和流匹配处理图像生成。需要推进这一方向，为交错生成设计统一的强化学习框架。

Method: 提出UniGRPO框架，将多模态生成过程建模为具有稀疏终端奖励的马尔可夫决策过程。采用极简主义方法避免过度设计，集成标准GRPO用于推理和FlowGRPO用于视觉合成。对FlowGRPO进行关键修改：消除无分类器引导以保持线性无分支展开，用速度场的MSE惩罚替换潜在KL惩罚。

Result: 实验表明，这种统一训练方法通过推理显著提高了图像生成质量，为未来完全交错模型的后续训练提供了稳健且可扩展的基线。

Conclusion: 提出的UniGRPO框架为交错生成任务提供了有效的统一强化学习解决方案，通过改进的FlowGRPO实现了可扩展性，为多轮交错生成模型的后训练奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Unified models capable of interleaved generation have emerged as a promising paradigm, with the community increasingly converging on autoregressive modeling for text and flow matching for image generation. To advance this direction, we propose a unified reinforcement learning framework tailored for interleaved generation. We validate our approach on its fundamental unit: a single round of reasoning-driven image generation, where the model first expands the user prompt through reasoning, followed by image synthesis. Formulating this multimodal generation process as a Markov Decision Process with sparse terminal rewards, we introduce UniGRPO to jointly optimize text and image generation policies using GRPO. Adopting a minimalist methodology to avoid over-design, we leverage established training recipes for both modalities by seamlessly integrating standard GRPO for reasoning and FlowGRPO for visual synthesis. To ensure scalability to multi-round interleaved generation, we introduce two critical modifications to the original FlowGRPO: (1) eliminating classifier-free guidance to maintain linear, unbranched rollouts, which is essential for scaling to complex scenarios involving multi-turn interactions and multi-condition generation (e.g., editing); and (2) replacing the standard latent KL penalty with an MSE penalty directly on the velocity fields, providing a more robust and direct regularization signal to mitigate reward hacking effectively. Our experiments demonstrate that this unified training recipe significantly enhances image generation quality through reasoning, providing a robust and scalable baseline for the future post-training of fully interleaved models.

</details>


### [136] [MedObvious: Exposing the Medical Moravec's Paradox in VLMs via Clinical Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23501)
*Ufaq Khan,Umair Nawaz,L D M S S Teja,Numaan Saeed,Muhammad Bilal,Yutong Xie,Mohammad Yaqub,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedObvious是一个包含1,880个任务的基准测试，专门用于评估医学视觉语言模型在输入验证方面的能力，即模型能否识别多面板图像集中的不一致或无效输入，这是临床诊断前的关键安全检查步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型基准测试假设输入验证问题已解决，忽略了模型可能在输入不一致或无效时仍生成看似合理的诊断文本这一关键失败模式。临床实践中，诊断前需要进行预诊断合理性检查，验证输入的模态、解剖结构、视角等是否有效。

Method: 研究者开发了MedObvious基准测试，包含1,880个任务，通过多面板图像集测试模型的集合级一致性能力。基准分为五个渐进层级：从基本方向/模态不匹配到临床解剖结构/视角验证和分流式线索。采用五种评估格式测试不同接口下的鲁棒性。

Result: 评估17个不同VLMs发现：合理性检查仍然不可靠；多个模型在正常（阴性对照）输入上产生幻觉异常；当扩展到更大图像集时性能下降；多项选择和开放式设置之间的准确率差异显著。

Conclusion: 预诊断验证对于医学VLMs仍未解决，应被视为部署前独立的安全关键能力。MedObvious基准测试揭示了当前模型在输入验证方面的不足，强调了这一能力的重要性。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used for tasks like medical report generation and visual question answering. However, fluent diagnostic text does not guarantee safe visual understanding. In clinical practice, interpretation begins with pre-diagnostic sanity checks: verifying that the input is valid to read (correct modality and anatomy, plausible viewpoint and orientation, and no obvious integrity violations). Existing benchmarks largely assume this step is solved, and therefore miss a critical failure mode: a model can produce plausible narratives even when the input is inconsistent or invalid. We introduce MedObvious, a 1,880-task benchmark that isolates input validation as a set-level consistency capability over small multi-panel image sets: the model must identify whether any panel violates expected coherence. MedObvious spans five progressive tiers, from basic orientation/modality mismatches to clinically motivated anatomy/viewpoint verification and triage-style cues, and includes five evaluation formats to test robustness across interfaces. Evaluating 17 different VLMs, we find that sanity checking remains unreliable: several models hallucinate anomalies on normal (negative-control) inputs, performance degrades when scaling to larger image sets, and measured accuracy varies substantially between multiple-choice and open-ended settings. These results show that pre-diagnostic verification remains unsolved for medical VLMs and should be treated as a distinct, safety-critical capability before deployment.

</details>


### [137] [OccAny: Generalized Unconstrained Urban 3D Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23502)
*Anh-Quan Cao,Tuan-Hung Vu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OccAny是首个无约束的城市3D占用模型，能够在无标定域外场景中从序列、单目或环视图像预测带分割特征的度量占用，通过分割强制和新视角渲染提升几何补全能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D占用预测方法依赖域内标注和精确传感器先验，在可扩展性和域外泛化能力上受限。虽然视觉几何基础模型展现出强泛化能力，但缺乏城市占用预测所需的关键要素：度量预测、杂乱场景中的几何补全以及城市场景适应。

Method: 提出首个广义3D占用框架OccAny，包含：(1)分割强制机制，通过强制分割一致性提升占用质量并实现掩码级预测；(2)新视角渲染流程，推断新视角几何以实现测试时视角增强，促进几何补全。

Result: 在3D占用预测任务上，OccAny超越所有视觉几何基线方法，同时在两个成熟的城市占用预测数据集上，在三种输入设置下与域内自监督方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: OccAny填补了视觉几何基础模型与城市占用预测需求之间的空白，实现了首个能够处理无标定域外场景的通用3D占用预测框架，具有优异的泛化能力和几何补全性能。

Abstract: Relying on in-domain annotations and precise sensor-rig priors, existing 3D occupancy prediction methods are limited in both scalability and out-of-domain generalization. While recent visual geometry foundation models exhibit strong generalization capabilities, they were mainly designed for general purposes and lack one or more key ingredients required for urban occupancy prediction, namely metric prediction, geometry completion in cluttered scenes and adaptation to urban scenarios. We address this gap and present OccAny, the first unconstrained urban 3D occupancy model capable of operating on out-of-domain uncalibrated scenes to predict and complete metric occupancy coupled with segmentation features. OccAny is versatile and can predict occupancy from sequential, monocular, or surround-view images. Our contributions are three-fold: (i) we propose the first generalized 3D occupancy framework with (ii) Segmentation Forcing that improves occupancy quality while enabling mask-level prediction, and (iii) a Novel View Rendering pipeline that infers novel-view geometry to enable test-time view augmentation for geometry completion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OccAny outperforms all visual geometry baselines on 3D occupancy prediction task, while remaining competitive with in-domain self-supervised methods across three input settings on two established urban occupancy prediction datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/valeoai/OccAny .

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [T-MAP: Red-Teaming LLM Agents with Trajectory-aware Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22341)
*Hyomin Lee,Sangwoo Park,Yumin Choi,Sohyun An,Seanie Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出T-MAP方法，通过轨迹感知的进化搜索自动生成对抗性提示，针对MCP环境中的LLM智能体进行红队测试，揭示传统文本攻击未能发现的工具执行漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试主要关注从大语言模型引出有害文本输出，但未能捕捉到在多步工具执行过程中出现的智能体特定漏洞，特别是在快速发展的Model Context Protocol（MCP）生态系统中。

Method: 提出轨迹感知进化搜索方法T-MAP，利用执行轨迹指导对抗性提示的发现。该方法能够自动生成不仅绕过安全防护机制，还能通过实际工具交互可靠实现有害目标的攻击。

Result: 在多种MCP环境中的实证评估表明，T-MAP在攻击实现率（ARR）上显著优于基线方法，并且对前沿模型（包括GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Pro、Qwen3.5和GLM-5）仍然有效。

Conclusion: T-MAP方法揭示了自主LLM智能体中先前未被充分探索的漏洞，表明传统文本攻击方法无法捕捉到在多步工具执行环境中出现的智能体特定安全风险。

Abstract: While prior red-teaming efforts have focused on eliciting harmful text outputs from large language models (LLMs), such approaches fail to capture agent-specific vulnerabilities that emerge through multi-step tool execution, particularly in rapidly growing ecosystems such as the Model Context Protocol (MCP). To address this gap, we propose a trajectory-aware evolutionary search method, T-MAP, which leverages execution trajectories to guide the discovery of adversarial prompts. Our approach enables the automatic generation of attacks that not only bypass safety guardrails but also reliably realize harmful objectives through actual tool interactions. Empirical evaluations across diverse MCP environments demonstrate that T-MAP substantially outperforms baselines in attack realization rate (ARR) and remains effective against frontier models, including GPT-5.2, Gemini-3-Pro, Qwen3.5, and GLM-5, thereby revealing previously underexplored vulnerabilities in autonomous LLM agents.

</details>


### [139] [mmFHE: mmWave Sensing with End-to-End Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22437)
*Tanvir Ahmed,Yixuan Gao,Adnan Armouti,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: mmFHE是首个实现毫米波雷达传感全同态加密的系统，可在边缘设备加密原始距离剖面，在不可信云上同态执行完整的毫米波信号处理和ML推理流水线，提供输入隐私和数据无感知性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达传感产生大量敏感生理和行为数据，传统云处理存在隐私泄露风险。需要在不暴露原始数据的情况下，在不可信云上执行完整的信号处理和机器学习推理。

Method: 设计七个可组合、数据无感知的同态加密内核，替代标准DSP例程，使用固定算术电路。这些内核可灵活组合成不同应用特定流水线，在心率监测和手势识别两个代表性任务上验证。

Result: 加密引入的误差可忽略：心率/呼吸率MAE <10^-3 bpm（相比明文），手势识别准确率84.5%（明文84.7%）。端到端云GPU延迟：10秒心率窗口103秒，3秒手势窗口37秒。

Conclusion: mmFHE证明在现有商用硬件上实现隐私保护的端到端毫米波传感是可行的，通过同态加密提供强大的密码学保证，有效抵御各种监督和非监督隐私攻击。

Abstract: We present mmFHE, the first system that enables fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) for end-to-end mmWave radar sensing. mmFHE encrypts raw range profiles on a lightweight edge device and executes the entire mmWave signal-processing and ML inference pipeline homomorphically on an untrusted cloud that operates exclusively on ciphertexts. At the core of mmFHE is a library of seven composable, data-oblivious FHE kernels that replace standard DSP routines with fixed arithmetic circuits. These kernels can be flexibly composed into different application-specific pipelines. We demonstrate this approach on two representative tasks: vital-sign monitoring and gesture recognition. We formally prove two cryptographic guarantees for any pipeline assembled from this library: input privacy, the cloud learns nothing about the sensor data; and data obliviousness, the execution trace is identical on the cloud regardless of the data being processed. These guarantees effectively neutralize various supervised and unsupervised privacy attacks on raw data, including re-identification and data-dependent privacy leakage. Evaluation on three public radar datasets (270 vital-sign recordings, 600 gesture trials) shows that encryption introduces negligible error: HR/RR MAE <10^-3 bpm versus plaintext, and 84.5% gesture accuracy (vs. 84.7% plaintext) with end-to-end cloud GPU latency of 103s for a 10s vital-sign window and 37s for a 3s gesture window. These results show that privacy-preserving end-to-end mmWave sensing is feasible on commodity hardware today.

</details>


### [140] [Architecture-Derived CBOMs for Cryptographic Migration: A Security-Aware Architecture Tradeoff Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22442)
*Eduard Hirsch,Kristina Raab*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SATAM方法通过架构评估生成具有安全上下文和迁移相关元数据的加密物料清单，提升加密迁移规划的信息可用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密物料清单通常基于工具或库存生成，缺乏架构意图、原理和安全上下文，限制了其在加密迁移规划中的实用性

Method: 提出安全感知架构权衡分析方法，整合ATAM、arc42、STRIDE、ADR和CARAF等方法，识别分析安全相关的加密决策点，并将其记录为明确的架构决策，使用CycloneDX兼容扩展为CBOM条目添加架构上下文、安全意图和迁移关键元数据

Result: 架构衍生的CBOM能够捕获迁移相关上下文，这些信息在基于库存的方法中通常缺失，从而提高了加密迁移规划和长期加密敏捷性所需信息的可用性

Conclusion: SATAM方法通过架构评估生成具有安全上下文和迁移相关元数据的加密物料清单，显著提升了加密迁移规划的信息可用性和长期加密敏捷性

Abstract: Cryptographic migration driven by algorithm deprecation, regulatory change, and post-quantum readiness requires more than an inventory of cryptographic assets. Existing Cryptographic Bills of Materials (CBOMs) are typically tool- or inventory-derived. They lack architectural intent, rationale, and security context, limiting their usefulness for migration planning. This paper introduces Security-Aware Architecture Tradeoff Analysis Method (SATAM), a security-aware adaptation of scenario-based architecture evaluation that derives an architecture-grounded, context-sensitive CBOM. SATAM integrates established approaches: ATAM, arc42, STRIDE, ADR, and CARAF. These are included to identify and analyze security-relevant cryptographic decision points and document them as explicit architectural decisions. These artifacts are used to annotate CBOM entries with architectural context, security intent, and migration-critical metadata using CycloneDX-compatible extensions. Following a Design Science Research approach, the paper presents the method design, a conceptual traceability model, and an illustrative application. The results demonstrate that architecture-derived CBOMs capture migration-relevant context that is typically absent from inventory-based approaches. Thereby, SATAM improves availability of information required for informed cryptographic migration planning and long-term cryptographic agility.

</details>


### [141] [BlindMarket: Enabling Verifiable, Confidential, and Traceable IP Core Distribution in Zero-Trust Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22685)
*Zhaoxiang Liu,Samuel Judson,Raj Dutta,Mark Santolucito,Xiaolong Guo,Ning Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BlindMarket是一个端到端的零信任硬件IP核分发框架，支持IP用户和供应商在交易前完成可验证性和保密性的IP交易，交易后提供可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决硬件IP核交易中的信任问题，在保护IP机密性的同时确保交易的可验证性，克服密码学协议可扩展性有限和硬件验证困难的问题。

Method: 提出验证启发式方法，采用基于影响锥的设计剪枝技术，结合控制流引导的启发式策略，构建端到端零信任分发框架。

Result: 在13个真实硬件IP核设计中成功验证了12个，通过设计剪枝和控制流引导启发式方法实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: BlindMarket框架能够有效处理多样化的真实硬件IP核交易，在保证安全性的同时提供良好的可扩展性和性能。

Abstract: We present BlindMarket, an end-to-end zero-trust distribution framework for hardware IP cores. BlindMarket allows two parties, the IP user and the IP vendor, to complete an IP trading process with strong guarantees of verifiability and confidentiality before the transaction, and then traceability after. We propose verification heuristics and adapt the cone of influence-based design pruning to overcome the limited scalability common to cryptographic protocols and the hardness of the underlying hardware verification. We systematically evaluate our framework on a diverse set of real-world hardware benchmarks, and the results demonstrate that BlindMarket effectively completes across a diverse set of real-world hardware IP cores, demonstrating successful verification on 12 out of 13 designs and substantial performance improvements enabled by design pruning and control-flow guided heuristics.

</details>


### [142] [Does Teaming-Up LLMs Improve Secure Code Generation? A Comprehensive Evaluation with Multi-LLMSecCodeEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22717)
*Bushra Sabir,Shigang Liu,Seung Ick Jang,Sharif Abuadbba,Yansong Gao,Kristen Moore,SangCheol Kim,Hyoungshick Kim,Surya Nepal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 多LLM集成与协作策略能显著提升安全代码生成，集成静态分析的集成管道比单LLM基线提升达47.3%，混合管道（集成、检测、修复）实现最强安全性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在自然语言生成源代码方面取得进展，但安全漏洞问题依然存在。本研究旨在系统评估多LLM集成和协作策略是否能有效改善安全代码生成

Method: 提出MULTI-LLMSECCODEEVAL框架，结合多个LLM、静态分析和结构化协作来评估和增强安全漏洞管理生命周期。使用SecLLMEval和SecLLMHolmes基准测试十个管道，涵盖单模型、集成、协作和混合设计

Result: 集成静态分析的集成管道在SecLLMEval上比单LLM基线提升47.3%，在SecLLMHolmes上提升19.3%。纯LLM协作管道提升较小（8.9%-22.3%）。混合管道（集成、检测、修复）表现最强，比最佳集成基线提升1.78%-4.72%，比协作基线提升19.81%-26.78%

Conclusion: 安全代码生成不依赖于模型规模，而是需要精心设计的多模型系统架构。小型、结构化的多模型集成持续优于大型单体LLM，表明安全代码来自精心编排的多模型系统设计而非规模效应

Abstract: Automatically generating source code from natural language using large language models (LLMs) is becoming common, yet security vulnerabilities persist despite advances in fine tuning and prompting. In this work, we systematically evaluate whether multi LLM ensembles and collaborative strategies can meaningfully improve secure code generation. We present MULTI-LLMSECCODEEVAL, a framework for assessing and enhancing security across the vulnerability management lifecycle by combining multiple LLMs with static analysis and structured collaboration. Using SecLLMEval and SecLLMHolmes, we benchmark ten pipelines spanning single model, ensemble, collaborative, and hybrid designs. Our results show that ensemble pipelines augmented with static analysis improve secure code generation over single LLM baselines by up to 47.3% on SecLLMEval and 19.3% on SecLLMHolmes, while purely LLM based collaborative pipelines yield smaller gains of 8.9% to 22.3%. Hybrid pipelines that integrate ensembling, detection, and patching achieve the strongest security performance, outperforming the best ensemble baseline by 1.78% to 4.72% and collaborative baselines by 19.81% to 26.78%. Ablation studies reveal that model scale alone does not ensure security. Smaller, structured multi model ensembles consistently outperform large monolithic LLMs. Overall, our findings demonstrate that secure code does not emerge from scale, but from carefully orchestrated multi model system design.

</details>


### [143] [CIPL: A Target-Independent Framework for Channel-Inversion Privacy Leakage in Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22751)
*Tao Huang,Chen Hou,Jiayang Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CIPL框架，将智能体系统中的隐私泄露建模为通道反转问题，支持跨目标攻击分析与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明大型语言模型智能体可能通过内部处理流程泄露敏感信息，但当前攻击方法局限于特定系统和输出表面。需要一种统一框架来系统研究智能体系统中的隐私泄露问题。

Method: 提出CIPL（Channel Inversion for Privacy Leakage）框架，将目标系统表示为包含敏感源、选择、组装、执行、观察和提取阶段的通用签名，并通过定位器、对齐器和多样化策略构建可重用的攻击语言。

Result: 研究结果表明隐私泄露不仅限于内存泄漏，而是取决于敏感内容如何路由到攻击者可见的观察通道。CIPL框架支持跨目标比较，同时保留目标特定的执行语义。

Conclusion: 智能体系统的隐私评估应从单一表面的攻击分析转向基于通道的信息暴露视角，CIPL为此提供了统一的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents may expose sensitive information through more than their final textual responses. Whenever private content is internally selected, assembled, and reused inside an agent pipeline, an attacker may attempt to turn that hidden dependence into an observable output signal. Existing evidence of this risk is strongest for memory leakage, but current attack formulations remain largely tied to specific systems and output surfaces. In this paper, we formulate privacy leakage in agentic systems as a \emph{channel inversion} problem and present CIPL (Channel Inversion for Privacy Leakage), a target-independent framework for studying such attacks. CIPL represents a target system through a common signature consisting of a sensitive source, selection, assembly, execution, observation, and extraction stages, and instantiates attacks through a reusable attack language built from a locator, an aligner, and a diversification policy. As a unified evaluation framework, CIPL supports cross-target comparison while preserving target-specific execution semantics. Our results provide initial evidence that privacy leakage is not confined to memory alone; instead, it depends on how sensitive content is routed into attacker-visible observation channels. These findings suggest that privacy evaluation for agentic systems should move beyond single-surface attack analysis toward a channel-oriented view of information exposure.

</details>


### [144] [Combinatorial Privacy: Private Multi-Party Bitstream Grand Sum by Hiding in Birkhoff Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22808)
*Praneeth Vepakomma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolyVeil协议使用Birkhoff多面体中的置换矩阵编码私有比特，实现k个客户端间的私有布尔求和。双层架构为服务器提供完美模拟安全，聚合器面临#P-hard推理问题。两种变体在隐私保护与计算复杂度间存在根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一个无需公钥基础设施、通信开销为O(k)且能输出精确聚合结果的私有布尔求和协议，同时探索在提供强安全保证（完美模拟安全）与实现非平凡差分隐私之间的可行性边界。

Method: 采用双层架构：服务器获得完美模拟安全（统计距离为零），聚合器面临基于永久值和混合判别式的#P-hard似然推断。开发了两种变体：完整变体（聚合器看到每个客户端的双随机矩阵）和压缩变体（聚合器看到单个标量）。使用有限样本(ε,δ)-DP分析，包含显式常数。

Result: 完整变体中，对数Lipschitz常数以n^4K_t增长，信号噪声分析显示DP保证仅在私有信号不可检测时非平凡。压缩变体中，单变量密度比在中等信噪比下产生非平凡ε，最优诱饵计数在CLT精度与噪声集中间平衡。协议无需PKI，通信开销O(k)，输出精确聚合。

Conclusion: 揭示了隐私保护与计算复杂度间的根本性张力：#P-hard性要求完整矩阵视图（可见Birkhoff结构），而非平凡DP要求标量视图（低维）。两者能否在同一变体中同时实现仍是开放问题。协议在实用性与理论保证间建立了新的权衡框架。

Abstract: We introduce PolyVeil, a protocol for private Boolean summation across $k$ clients that encodes private bits as permutation matrices in the Birkhoff polytope. A two-layer architecture gives the server perfect simulation-based security (statistical distance zero) while a separate aggregator faces \#P-hard likelihood inference via the permanent and mixed discriminant. Two variants (full and compressed) differ in what the aggregator observes.
  We develop a finite-sample $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP analysis with explicit constants. In the full variant, where the aggregator sees a doubly stochastic matrix per client, the log-Lipschitz constant grows as $n^4 K_t$ and a signal-to-noise analysis shows the DP guarantee is non-vacuous only when the private signal is undetectable. In the compressed variant, where the aggregator sees a single scalar, the univariate density ratio yields non-vacuous $\varepsilon$ at moderate SNR, with the optimal decoy count balancing CLT accuracy against noise concentration.
  This exposes a fundamental tension. \#P-hardness requires the full matrix view (Birkhoff structure visible), while non-vacuous DP requires the scalar view (low dimensionality). Whether both hold simultaneously in one variant remains open. The protocol needs no PKI, has $O(k)$ communication, and outputs exact aggregates.

</details>


### [145] [SoK: The Attack Surface of Agentic AI -- Tools, and Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22928)
*Ali Dehghantanha,Sajad Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了基于大语言模型的智能体系统的安全风险，提出了攻击分类、防御措施和安全检查清单，旨在帮助研究者和工程师理解和缓解智能体AI的安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从单纯的语言模型发展为结合工具、外部知识（RAG）和自主多智能体决策的智能体范式，其能力大幅扩展的同时也极大地扩大了攻击面。当前缺乏对智能体LLM系统安全风险的系统性梳理，需要为研究者和工程师提供全面的安全威胁分析和防御指南。

Method: 通过系统化方法，构建了智能体LLM系统的信任边界和安全风险分类体系。进行了详细的文献综述，综合分析了2023-2025年间超过20项同行评审研究、行业报告和标准。定义了攻击者模型和威胁场景，提出了安全评估指标（如不安全行动率、权限提升距离）。评估了各种防御措施的有效性，并为实践者制定了分阶段的安全检查清单。

Result: 研究发现智能体系统引入了新的攻击向量，包括间接提示注入、代码执行漏洞、RAG索引污染和跨智能体操纵等超越传统AI威胁的风险。现有的防御措施（输入净化、检索过滤器、沙箱、访问控制、AI护栏）在某些方面有效，但在保护方面仍存在不足。提出了具体的防御控制措施和部署智能体AI的安全检查清单。

Conclusion: 智能体AI系统带来了新的安全挑战，需要系统性的安全方法。论文为研究者和工程师提供了全面的安全风险分析和缓解指南，同时指出了安全自主AI领域的研究挑战，包括鲁棒的工具API、可验证的智能体行为、供应链安全保障等，并讨论了伦理和负责任披露实践。

Abstract: Recent AI systems combine large language models with tools, external knowledge via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and even autonomous multi-agent decision loops. This agentic AI paradigm greatly expands capabilities - but also vastly enlarges the attack surface. In this systematization, we map out the trust boundaries and security risks of agentic LLM-based systems. We develop a comprehensive taxonomy of attacks spanning prompt-level injections, knowledge-base poisoning, tool/plug-in exploits, and multi-agent emergent threats. Through a detailed literature review, we synthesize evidence from 2023-2025, including more than 20 peer-reviewed and archival studies, industry reports, and standards. We find that agentic systems introduce new vectors for indirect prompt injection, code execution exploits, RAG index poisoning, and cross-agent manipulation that go beyond traditional AI threats. We define attacker models and threat scenarios, and propose metrics (e.g., Unsafe Action Rate, Privilege Escalation Distance) to evaluate security posture. Our survey examines defenses such as input sanitization, retrieval filters, sandboxes, access control, and "AI guardrails," assessing their effectiveness and pointing out the areas where protection is still lacking. To assist practitioners, we outline defensive controls and provide a phased security checklist for deploying agentic AI (covering design-time hardening, runtime monitoring, and incident response). Finally, we outline open research challenges in secure autonomous AI (robust tool APIs, verifiable agent behavior, supply-chain safeguards) and discuss ethical and responsible disclosure practices. We systematize recent findings to help researchers and engineers understand and mitigate security risks in agentic AI.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond Theoretical Bounds: Empirical Privacy Loss Calibration for Text Rewriting Under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22968)
*Weijun Li,Arnaud Grivet Sébert,Qiongkai Xu,Annabelle McIver,Mark Dras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TeDA框架通过假设检验方法实证校准LDP文本重写机制，表明名义ε值相似的机制在实际可区分性上差异显著，为隐私-效用权衡评估提供更可比的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的广泛应用，如何在隐私保护前提下共享文本数据成为重要挑战。现有基于本地差分隐私(LDP)的文本重写方法使用ε参数提供形式化隐私保证，但名义ε值难以解释且在不同机制间缺乏可比性，需要实证校准方法。

Method: 提出TeDA框架，采用假设检验方法在表面空间和嵌入空间中实例化文本可区分性审计。该方法通过实证评估从隐私化文本中区分原始文本的能力，实现对LDP文本重写机制的校准。

Result: 对多个代表性机制应用校准后发现，相似的名义ε界限可能对应完全不同的可区分性水平。实证校准为隐私-效用权衡评估提供了更可比较的基础，并成为实际LDP文本重写部署中机制比较和分析的实用工具。

Conclusion: TeDA框架通过实证校准解决了LDP文本重写机制中名义ε值可比性不足的问题，为实际部署中的隐私保护效果评估和机制选择提供了更可靠的依据，有助于优化隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models has increased interest in sharing textual data in a privacy-preserving manner. One prominent line of work addresses this challenge through text rewriting under Local Differential Privacy (LDP), where input texts are locally obfuscated before release with formal privacy guarantees. These guarantees are typically expressed by a parameter $\varepsilon$ that upper bounds the worst-case privacy loss. However, nominal $\varepsilon$ values are often difficult to interpret and compare across mechanisms. In this work, we investigate how to empirically calibrate across text rewriting mechanisms under LDP. We propose TeDA, which formulates calibration via a hypothesis-testing framework that instantiates text distinguishability audits in both surface and embedding spaces, enabling empirical assessment of indistinguishability from privatized texts. Applying this calibration to several representative mechanisms, we demonstrate that similar nominal $\varepsilon$ bounds can imply very different levels of distinguishability. Empirical calibration thus provides a more comparable footing for evaluating privacy-utility trade-offs, as well as a practical tool for mechanism comparison and analysis in real-world LDP text rewriting deployments.

</details>


### [147] [How Far Should We Need to Go : Evaluate Provenance-based Intrusion Detection Systems in Industrial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22982)
*Yue Xiao,Ling Jiang,Sen Nie,Ding Li,Shi Wu,Ke Xu,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估了溯源入侵检测系统在工业场景中的性能，发现现有系统存在跨主机/平台移植性差、对真实攻击检测性能低、误报率高等问题，并提出改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然溯源入侵检测系统在原始论文评估中表现良好，但其在工业场景中的实际性能尚不明确。现有研究主要基于DARPA数据集进行评估，而工业场景数据具有异构多源输入、攻击者能力更强、良性活动复杂度增加等新特征，需要系统评估现有系统在真实工业环境中的表现。

Method: 1. 分析DARPA数据集与工业场景数据的差异，识别工业数据的三个新特征；2. 构建多个数据集用于评估五种最先进的溯源入侵检测系统；3. 通过系统评估揭示现有系统的问题；4. 基于评估结果和工业实践提供问题解决思路；5. 提出降低误报率的方法和系统改进建议。

Result: 评估发现现有溯源入侵检测系统面临三大挑战：1. 跨不同主机和平台的移植性差；2. 对真实世界攻击的检测性能低；3. 随着良性活动变化，误报率居高不下。提出的误报缓解方法能将人工工作量减少2/3。

Conclusion: 溯源入侵检测系统在工业场景中面临显著挑战，需要针对工业数据的特征进行改进。论文提出的见解和建议为未来研究提供了方向，特别是需要开发更具适应性和可移植性的系统来应对真实工业环境中的复杂威胁。

Abstract: Provenance-based Intrusion Detection Systems (PIDSes) have been widely used to detect Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). Although many studies achieve high performance in the evaluations of their original papers, their performance in industrial scenarios remains unclear. To fill this gap, we conduct the first systematic evaluation and analysis of PIDSes in industrial scenarios. We first analyze the differences between the data from DARPA datasets and that collected in industrial scenarios, identifying three main new characteristics in industry: heterogeneous multi-source inputs, more powerful attackers, and increasing benign activity complexity. We then build several datasets to evaluate five state-of-the-art PIDSes. The evaluation results reveal challenges for existing PIDSes, including poor portability across different hosts and platforms, low detection performance against real-world attacks, and high false positive rates with ever-changing benign activities. Based on the evaluation results and our industrial practices, we provide several insights to solve or explain the above problems. For example, we propose a method to mitigate the high false positives, which reduces manual effort by 2/3. Finally, we propose several research suggestions to improve PIDSes.

</details>


### [148] [A Critical Review on the Effectiveness and Privacy Threats of Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22987)
*Najeeb Jebreel,David Sánchez,Josep Domingo-Ferrer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一个评估框架来定义成员推理攻击构成真正隐私威胁的条件，并基于此框架评估代表性攻击，发现在现实条件下这些攻击仅代表弱隐私威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推理攻击已成为衡量机器学习隐私泄露的事实标准，但现有研究可能高估了其实际威胁程度，导致过度防御和模型效用的不必要牺牲。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，明确定义成员推理攻击构成真正隐私威胁的条件，并在此框架下系统性地评估代表性成员推理攻击方法。

Result: 在框架定义的现实条件下，成员推理攻击仅代表弱隐私威胁，表明将其作为隐私度量标准可能导致风险高估和过度防御。

Conclusion: 成员推理攻击的实际隐私威胁被高估，需要更准确的评估框架来避免不必要的防御措施和模型效用损失。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) aim to determine whether a data sample was included in a machine learning (ML) model's training set and have become the de facto standard for measuring privacy leakages in ML. We propose an evaluation framework that defines the conditions under which MIAs constitute a genuine privacy threat, and review representative MIAs against it. We find that, under the realistic conditions defined in our framework, MIAs represent weak privacy threats. Thus, relying on them as a privacy metric in ML can lead to an overestimation of risk and to unnecessary sacrifices in model utility as a consequence of employing too strong defenses.

</details>


### [149] [Multi-User Multi-Key Image Steganography with Key Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23005)
*Tzu-Ti Wei,Yu-Han Tseng,Jun-Yi Lin,Yu-Chee Tseng,Jen-Jee Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PUSNet-MK：PUSNet的多密钥扩展，通过不匹配密钥隔离损失实现严格密钥隔离，防止跨密钥解码，解决了PUSNet在单密钥对多用户场景下的安全限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PUSNet在正常操作时执行图像净化，触发时进行隐写嵌入，但仅支持单用户单密钥对，限制了在多用户场景下的应用。需要解决多用户设置中的密钥隔离和跨密钥解码安全问题。

Method: 提出PUSNet-MK多密钥扩展，通过不匹配密钥隔离损失强制实现严格密钥隔离，当应用错误密钥时有效防止跨密钥解码，同时保持预期的隐写行为。

Result: 大量实验表明，PUSNet-MK能生成高质量隐写图像并实现准确的秘密恢复，同时防止意外信息泄露，解决了PUSNet的关键安全限制。

Conclusion: PUSNet-MK成功扩展了PUSNet的多密钥能力，通过严格的密钥隔离机制解决了多用户场景下的安全限制，保持了隐写系统的视觉保真度和可靠恢复能力。

Abstract: Steganography conceals secret information within innocuous carriers while preserving visual fidelity and enabling reliable recovery. Recent unified networks operate normally under untriggered conditions but switch to hidden steganographic tasks when triggered. PUSNet follows this paradigm by performing image purification during normal operation and steganographic embedding when activated. However, it supports only a single user with one key pair, limiting its applicability in multi-user settings.
