We propose PUSNet-MK, a multi-key extension that enforces strict key isolation via a mismatched-key isolation loss, effectively preventing cross-key decoding when a wrong key is applied. This design preserves the intended steganographic behavior while addressing a critical security limitation of PUSNet. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PUSNet-MK produces high-quality stego images and accurate secret recovery, while preventing unintended information leakage.

</details>


### [150] [TRAP: Hijacking VLA CoT-Reasoning via Adversarial Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23117)
*Zhengxian Huang,Wenjun Zhu,Haoxuan Qiu,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TRAP攻击框架，利用对抗性补丁（如杯垫）劫持基于思维链推理的视觉-语言-动作模型，在不修改用户指令的情况下实现目标控制劫持


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然结合思维链推理的VLA模型在机器人操作中展现出强大的泛化能力和可解释性，但其安全性尚未得到充分研究。论文发现思维链推理引入了一个新的攻击向量，可能导致目标控制劫持，例如让机器人错误地将刀具递给人而不是苹果

Method: 提出TRAP攻击框架：1）通过实证证据表明思维链强烈控制动作生成，即使与输入指令语义不一致；2）使用对抗性补丁（如放置在桌上的杯垫）来破坏中间思维链推理；3）通过优化思维链对抗损失，诱导特定的攻击者定义行为

Result: 在3种主流VLA架构和3种思维链推理范式上进行广泛评估，验证了TRAP的有效性。在实际环境中通过打印在纸上的补丁实现了攻击，证明了现实世界中的可行性

Conclusion: 思维链推理在VLA系统中引入了新的安全漏洞，TRAP攻击框架展示了通过对抗性补丁劫持思维链推理的可行性。研究结果强调了保护VLA系统中思维链推理安全的紧迫性

Abstract: By integrating Chain-of-Thought(CoT) reasoning, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, particularly by improving generalization and interpretability. However, the security of CoT-based reasoning mechanisms remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we show that CoT reasoning introduces a novel attack vector for targeted control hijacking--for example, causing a robot to mistakenly deliver a knife to a person instead of an apple--without modifying the user's instruction. We first provide empirical evidence that CoT strongly governs action generation, even when it is semantically misaligned with the input instructions. Building on this observation, we propose TRAP, the first targeted adversarial attack framework for CoT-reasoning VLA models. TRAP uses an adversarial patch (e.g., a coaster placed on the table) to corrupt intermediate CoT reasoning and hijack the VLA's output. By optimizing the CoT adversarial loss, TRAP induces specific and adversary-defined behaviors. Extensive evaluations across 3 mainstream VLA architectures and 3 CoT reasoning paradigms validate the effectiveness of TRAP. Notably, we implemented the patch by printing it on paper in a real-world setting. Our findings highlight the urgent need to secure CoT reasoning in VLA systems.

</details>


### [151] [Robust Safety Monitoring of Language Models via Activation Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23171)
*Toluwani Aremu,Daniil Ognev,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种针对大语言模型监控的鲁棒防御方法——激活水印，以对抗能够规避检测的自适应攻击者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能被滥用来泄露敏感信息，现有监控机制在面对知道监控算法的自适应攻击者时存在脆弱性。由于LLM提供商无法修补其安全机制，需要设计能够抵御自适应攻击的鲁棒监控方案。

Method: 将鲁棒LLM监控建模为安全博弈，提出激活水印方法，通过在推理过程中谨慎引入不确定性来防御自适应攻击者。攻击者知道监控算法但不知道密钥，从而无法有效规避检测。

Result: 实验表明现有LLM监控器对自适应攻击者存在漏洞，而激活水印方法在自适应攻击下比基线防御方法性能提升高达52%。

Conclusion: 激活水印是一种有效的鲁棒监控防御机制，能够显著提升大语言模型在对抗知道监控算法的自适应攻击者时的安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be misused to reveal sensitive information, such as weapon-making instructions or writing malware. LLM providers rely on $\emph{monitoring}$ to detect and flag unsafe behavior during inference. An open security challenge is $\emph{adaptive}$ adversaries who craft attacks that simultaneously (i) evade detection while (ii) eliciting unsafe behavior. Adaptive attackers are a major concern as LLM providers cannot patch their security mechanisms, since they are unaware of how their models are being misused. We cast $\emph{robust}$ LLM monitoring as a security game, where adversaries who know about the monitor try to extract sensitive information, while a provider must accurately detect these adversarial queries at low false positive rates. Our work (i) shows that existing LLM monitors are vulnerable to adaptive attackers and (ii) designs improved defenses through $\emph{activation watermarking}$ by carefully introducing uncertainty for the attacker during inference. We find that $\emph{activation watermarking}$ outperforms guard baselines by up to $52\%$ under adaptive attackers who know the monitoring algorithm but not the secret key.

</details>


### [152] [Privacy-Aware Smart Cameras: View Coverage via Socially Responsible Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23197)
*Chuhao Qin,Lukas Esterle,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出去中心化智能摄像头协调框架，通过集体学习自主选择朝向，在监控公共空间的同时排除隐私敏感区域，实现覆盖效率与隐私保护的平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决城市智能监控中覆盖效率与隐私保护的矛盾，避免过度依赖昂贵加密技术，实现社会责任的智能城市监控系统

Method: 去中心化框架，智能摄像头通过交互协调和集体学习自主选择朝向，采用软硬约束满足消除隐私侵犯，无需集中控制

Result: 实验显示覆盖效率比基线方法提高18.42%，隐私侵犯降低85.53%，可扩展到数百至数千个摄像头

Conclusion: 该框架为运营商和政策制定者提供了实用指南，展示了视野范围、空间布局和预算如何共同影响大规模敏感城市环境中的覆盖效率和隐私保护

Abstract: Coordination of view coverage via privacy-aware smart cameras is key to a more socially responsible urban intelligence. Rather than maximizing view coverage at any cost or over relying on expensive cryptographic techniques, we address how cameras can coordinate to legitimately monitor public spaces while excluding privacy-sensitive regions by design. This article proposes a decentralized framework in which interactive smart cameras coordinate to autonomously select their orientation via collective learning, while eliminating privacy violations via soft and hard constraint satisfaction. The approach scales to hundreds up to thousands of cameras without any centralized control. Experimental evidence shows 18.42% higher coverage efficiency and 85.53% lower privacy violation than baselines and other state-of-the-art approaches. This significant advance further unravels practical guidelines for operators and policymakers: how the field of view, spatial placement, and budget of cameras operating by ethically-aligned artificial intelligence jointly influence coverage efficiency and privacy protection in large-scale and sensitive urban environments.

</details>


### [153] [PRETTINESS -- Privacy pResErving aTTrIbute maNagEment SyStem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23221)
*Jelizaveta Vakarjuk,Alisa Pankova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个用于欧洲数字身份钱包的通用凭证撤销系统，基于单服务器和安全传输通道，在通用可组合模型中证明安全性，并通过概念验证实现评估效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲数字身份钱包需要撤销已签发凭证的能力，虽然可以签发短期凭证，但在某些情况下不方便，需要独立的撤销服务来随时撤销凭证

Method: 提出完整的端到端通用凭证撤销系统描述，技术上依赖单个服务器和各方之间的安全传输通道，在通用可组合模型中证明安全性

Result: 基于概念验证实现评估系统效率，证明所提撤销功能的安全性

Conclusion: 为欧洲数字身份钱包提供了一个安全、高效的通用凭证撤销系统解决方案

Abstract: European Digital Identity (EUDI) Wallet aims to provide end users with a way to get attested credentials from issuers, and present them to different relying parties. An important property mentioned in the regulatory frameworks is the possibility to revoke a previously issued credential. While it is possible to issue a short-lived credential, in some cases it may be inconvenient, and a separate revocation service which allows to revoke a credential at any time may be necessary. In this work, we propose a full end-to-end description of a generic credential revocation system, which technically relies on a single server and secure transmission channels between parties. We prove security of the proposed revocation functionality in the universal composability model, and estimate its efficiency based on a proof-of-concept implementation.

</details>


### [154] [Gyokuro: Source-assisted Private Membership Testing using Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23226)
*Yoshimichi Nakatsuka,Nicolas Dutly,Kari Kostiainen,Srdjan Capkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Gyokuro是一个基于可信执行环境(TEE)的源辅助私有成员测试(SPMT)方案，通过证明服务器已足够进展将数据项添加到数据库，而非证明数据项存在，实现了高效、可扩展且隐私保护的成员测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统私有成员测试(PMT)协议在效率、可扩展性和隐私保护方面存在挑战。源辅助PMT(SPMT)在证书透明性和供应链审计等应用中很重要，但需要设计一个既高效、可扩展又能保护隐私的方案。

Method: Gyokuro采用与传统成员测试不同的方法：利用可信执行环境(TEE)生成证明，证明服务器已足够进展将数据项添加到数据库，而非证明数据项存在。借助现有监控服务，客户端可以推断没有数据项被从数据库中移除。这种方法使成员测试查询不包含任何关于客户端兴趣的信息，消除了复杂低效的保护机制需求。

Result: Gyokuro实现了7毫秒的成员测试延迟和约1400请求/秒/核心的吞吐量。通信和计算需求与数据库大小无关，支持大规模数据库的成员测试。提供了强大的隐私保证，因为客户端查询不泄露其兴趣信息。

Conclusion: Gyokuro通过创新的TEE-based方法解决了SPMT的效率、可扩展性和隐私保护挑战，实现了实用的高性能成员测试，适用于证书透明性和供应链审计等应用场景。

Abstract: Private Membership Testing (PMT) protocols enable clients to verify whether a certain data item is included in a database without revealing the item to the database operator or other external parties. This paper examines Source-assisted PMT (SPMT), in which clients leverage compact data source-provided information issued when the data item is first submitted to the database. SPMT is relevant in applications such as certificate transparency and supply-chain auditing; yet, designing an approach that is efficient, scalable, and privacy-preserving remains a challenge.
  This work presents Gyokuro, which takes a different approach to conventional membership testing schemes. Instead of requesting the server to produce a proof attesting that a certain data item exists in the database, we leverage Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) to produce proofs demonstrating that the server has made enough progress to add the data item to the database. With the help of existing monitoring services, clients can infer that no items have been removed from the database. This allows Gyokuro to provide strong privacy guaranties and achieve high efficiency, as a client's membership testing query does not include any information regarding their interests, and eliminates the need for complex and inefficient protection mechanisms. Additionally, this approach enables membership testing on large-scale databases, since the communication and computation required are independent of the database size. Our evaluations show practical feasibility, achieving 7 ms membership testing latency and throughput of around 1400 requests/sec/core.

</details>


### [155] [The Power of Power Codes: New Classes of Easy Instances for the Linear Equivalence Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23230)
*Michele Battagliola,Anna-Lena Horlemann,Abhinaba Mazumder,Rocco Mora,Paolo Santini,Michael Schaller,Violetta Weger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将利用Schur积解决置换等价问题(PEP)的方法扩展到线性等价问题(LEP)，通过幂码、Frobenius自同构和Hermitian壳等代数工具识别了大量易解的LEP实例，并改进了当单项矩阵系数在有限域乘法子群时的PEP约简方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性等价问题(LEP)和置换等价问题(PEP)作为后量子密码方案(如LESS)的安全基础，近年来重新受到关注。先前工作已利用Schur积成功解决PEP，识别了许多易解实例。本文旨在将这一方法扩展到更一般的LEP，利用代数弱点解决该问题。

Method: 1. 推广Schur积方法到LEP；2. 引入幂码(power codes)概念；3. 结合Frobenius自同构和Hermitian壳；4. 识别多个易解LEP实例类别；5. 改进当单项矩阵系数在有限域乘法子群时的PEP约简方法。

Result: 成功将PEP的Schur积方法扩展到LEP，首次利用代数弱点解决LEP问题。通过幂码、Frobenius自同构和Hermitian壳的组合，识别了大量易解的LEP实例类别。同时改进了特定条件下的PEP约简方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次展示了如何利用代数弱点解决线性等价问题，扩展了先前仅适用于置换等价问题的方法。通过引入幂码概念并结合Frobenius自同构和Hermitian壳，为LEP提供了新的解决途径，对后量子密码方案的安全性分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: Given two linear codes, the Linear Equivalence Problem (LEP) asks to find (if it exists) a linear isometry between them; as a special case, we have the Permutation Equivalence Problem (PEP), in which isometries must be permutations. LEP and PEP have recently gained renewed interest as the security foundations for several post-quantum schemes, including LESS. A recent paper has introduced the use of the Schur product to solve PEP, identifying many new easy-to-solve instances. In this paper, we extend this result to LEP. In particular, we generalize the approach and rely on the more general notion of power codes. Combining it with Frobenius automorphisms and Hermitian hulls, we identify many classes of easy LEP instances. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work exploiting algebraic weaknesses for LEP. Finally we show an improved reduction to PEP whenever the coefficients of the monomial matrix are in a subgroup of the multiplicative group of the finite field.

</details>


### [156] [On the Vulnerability of FHE Computation to Silent Data Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23253)
*Jianan Mu,Ge Yu,Zhaoxuan Kan,Song Bian,Liang Kong,Zizhen Liu,Cheng Liu,Jing Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文全面评估了全同态加密（FHE）计算中的静默数据损坏（SDCs），通过大规模故障注入实验和理论分析揭示了FHE对瞬态硬件故障的脆弱性，并评估了不同容错机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FHE技术逐渐走向实际部署，其在真实硬件上的计算可靠性问题尚未得到充分探索。FHE相比明文计算具有更高的计算开销，使其更容易受到瞬态硬件故障的影响，且由于服务方无法访问明文数据，数据损坏可能保持静默状态而无法被察觉。

Method: 采用大规模故障注入实验来表征FHE对瞬态故障的脆弱性，同时通过理论分析错误传播行为来深入理解这种脆弱性的算法机制，并评估不同容错机制对缓解这些故障的有效性。

Result: 研究揭示了FHE计算对硬件故障的高度敏感性，静默数据损坏可能在不被察觉的情况下发生，通过理论分析深入理解了错误传播机制，并评估了不同容错策略在保护FHE计算完整性方面的效果。

Conclusion: FHE在实际硬件部署中面临显著的可靠性挑战，需要专门的容错机制来确保计算完整性。该研究为FHE系统的可靠性设计和评估提供了重要基础，强调了在隐私保护计算中考虑硬件故障影响的重要性。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) is rapidly emerging as a promising foundation for privacy-preserving cloud services, enabling computation directly on encrypted data. As FHE implementations mature and begin moving toward practical deployment in domains such as secure finance, biomedical analytics, and privacy-preserving AI, a critical question remains insufficiently explored: how reliable is FHE computation on real hardware? This question is especially important because, compared with plaintext computation, FHE incurs much higher computational overhead, making it more susceptible to transient hardware faults. Moreover, data corruptions are likely to remain silent: the FHE service has no access to the underlying plaintext, causing unawareness even though the corresponding decrypted result has already been corrupted. To this end, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of SDCs in FHE ciphertext computation. Through large-scale fault-injection experiments, we characterize the vulnerability of FHE to transient faults, and through a theoretical analysis of error-propagation behaviors, we gain deeper algorithmic insight into the mechanisms underlying this vulnerability. We further assess the effectiveness of different fault-tolerance mechanisms for mitigating these faults.

</details>


### [157] [Canonical Byte-String Encoding for Finite-Ring Cryptosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23364)
*Kyrylo Riabov,Serhii Kryvyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为base-m长度编解码器的规范映射，用于将字节字符串转换为模m的余数列表，为环映射协议提供标准化的字节到余数转换层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环映射协议在进行代数加密步骤之前需要一个规范的字节到余数转换层。本文旨在分离并标准化这一基础转换层，为后续加密操作提供可靠的数据表示基础。

Method: 基于Duda提出的rANS系统进行改进和适配，设计了base-m长度编解码器，实现了从长度小于2^64的字节字符串到模m余数列表的规范映射。编码器包含固定宽度的头部信息，解码对所有满足参数界限的模数都是精确的。

Result: 开发了Rust实现、Lean 4形式化验证和性能基准测试。形式化验证证明了固定宽度前缀可逆性、有效载荷状态边界低于2^64、流级往返正确性，以及所有输出符号都是有效的模m余数。

Conclusion: 该编解码器为环映射协议提供了标准化的字节到余数转换层，具有精确解码、容错性（可容忍附加的有效后缀数字）和形式化验证的可靠性。最后讨论了实际应用中的复杂性和实际考虑因素。

Abstract: Ring-mapping protocols need a canonical byte-to-residue layer before any algebraic encryption step can begin. This paper isolates that layer and presents the base-m length codec, a canonical map from byte strings of length less than 2^64 to lists of residues modulo m. The encoder builds on and adapts an rANS-based system proposed by Duda. Decoding is exact for all moduli satisfying the paper's parameter bounds. Because the encoding carries the byte length in its fixed-width header, decoding is also tolerant to appended valid suffix digits. The paper is accompanied by a Rust implementation of the described protocol, a Lean 4 formalization of the abstract codec with machine-checked proofs, and performance benchmarks. The Lean 4 formalization establishes fixed-width prefix inversion and payload-state bounds below 2^64, stream-level roundtrip correctness, and that every emitted symbol is a valid residue modulo m.
  We conclude with a complexity analysis and a discussion of practical considerations arising in real-world use of the codec.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Demystifying Low-Rank Knowledge Distillation in Large Language Models: Convergence, Generalization, and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22355)
*Alberlucia Rafael Soarez,Daniel Kim,Mariana Costa,Alejandro Torre*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为语言模型中的低秩知识蒸馏建立了理论框架，证明了优化动态的保持性、收敛速率、泛化边界，并提供了信息论分析和秩选择指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管低秩知识蒸馏方法（如LRC）在压缩大型语言模型方面取得了显著经验成功，但其理论基础仍然薄弱，缺乏对优化动态、收敛速率、泛化边界和秩选择原则的理论理解。

Method: 建立低秩知识蒸馏的严格理论框架，包括：1) 证明在温和假设下低秩投影保持优化动态；2) 推导显式收敛速率；3) 建立模型压缩与泛化能力之间的基本权衡的泛化边界；4) 提供激活克隆机制的信息论分析；5) 推导秩选择的数学指导原则。

Result: 理论结果包括：1) 收敛速率为O(1/√T)；2) 泛化误差随秩参数r的缩放为O(r(m+n)/√n)；3) 揭示了激活克隆机制在最大化师生中间表示互信息中的作用；4) 提出了最优秩选择r* = O(√n)的数学建议。实验验证表明经验收敛、秩缩放和泛化行为与理论边界高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为低秩知识蒸馏建立了坚实的理论基础，提供了对优化动态、收敛速率、泛化边界和秩选择原则的严格数学理解，为高效语言模型压缩提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation has emerged as a powerful technique for compressing large language models (LLMs) into efficient, deployable architectures while preserving their advanced capabilities. Recent advances in low-rank knowledge distillation, particularly methods like Low-Rank Clone (LRC), have demonstrated remarkable empirical success, achieving comparable performance to full-parameter distillation with significantly reduced training data and computational overhead. However, the theoretical foundations underlying these methods remain poorly understood. In this paper, we establish a rigorous theoretical framework for low-rank knowledge distillation in language models. We prove that under mild assumptions, low-rank projection preserves the optimization dynamics, yielding explicit convergence rates of $O(1/\sqrt{T})$. We derive generalization bounds that characterize the fundamental trade-off between model compression and generalization capability, showing that the generalization error scales with the rank parameter as $O(r(m+n)/\sqrt{n})$. Furthermore, we provide an information-theoretic analysis of the activation cloning mechanism, revealing its role in maximizing the mutual information between the teacher's and student's intermediate representations. Our theoretical results offer principled guidelines for rank selection, mathematically suggesting an optimal rank $r^* = O(\sqrt{n})$ where $n$ is the sample size. Experimental validation on standard language modeling benchmarks confirms our theoretical predictions, demonstrating that the empirical convergence, rank scaling, and generalization behaviors align closely with our bounds.

</details>


### [159] [SPDE Methods for Nonparametric Bayesian Posterior Contraction and Laplace Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22468)
*Enric Alberola-Boloix,Ioar Casado-Telletxea*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 扩展Mou等人(2024)的扩散框架到无限维空间，推导非参数贝叶斯模型的后验收缩率和有限样本Bernstein von Mises结果，通过Langevin SPDE表示后验，建立定量Laplace近似


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维扩散框架扩展到无限维非参数贝叶斯模型，建立后验收缩率和BvM结果的统一理论框架，解决非参数贝叶斯推断中的理论分析问题

Method: 将后验表示为可分Hilbert空间上Langevin随机偏微分方程的平稳测度，在多种似然曲率和正则性条件下控制后验矩，获得Hilbert范数下的非渐近浓度率，建立后验的定量Laplace近似

Result: 推导出非参数贝叶斯模型的后验收缩率，获得有限样本Bernstein von Mises结果，在非参数线性高斯逆问题中验证理论的有效性

Conclusion: 成功将扩散框架扩展到无限维设置，为分析非参数贝叶斯模型提供了统一的理论工具，能够控制后验矩并获得非渐近浓度率，Laplace近似为后验分析提供了实用方法

Abstract: We derive posterior contraction rates (PCRs) and finite-sample Bernstein von Mises (BvM) results for non-parametric Bayesian models by extending the diffusion-based framework of Mou et al. (2024) to the infinite-dimensional setting. The posterior is represented as the invariant measure of a Langevin stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE) on a separable Hilbert space, which allows us to control posterior moments and obtain non-asymptotic concentration rates in Hilbert norms under various likelihood curvature and regularity conditions. We also establish a quantitative Laplace approximation for the posterior. The theory is illustrated in a nonparametric linear Gaussian inverse problem.

</details>


### [160] [Privacy-Preserving Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback via Decoupled Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22563)
*Young Hyun Cho,Will Wei Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种用于人类反馈强化学习的差分隐私框架，仅对奖励学习施加隐私保护，从私有奖励模型推导最终策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好微调已成为训练大语言模型的重要环节，该阶段数据可能包含敏感用户信息，需要设计适合人类反馈强化学习结构的差分隐私管道

Method: 提出隐私保护框架，仅对奖励学习施加差分隐私，从得到的私有奖励模型推导最终策略。理论上分析次优性差距，建立极小极大下界

Result: 理论分析显示隐私贡献了超出通常非私有统计误差的额外加性项。实证中，合成实验验证了理论预测的缩放规律，在Anthropic HH-RLHF数据集上使用Gemma-2B-IT模型显示比现有差分隐私基线方法更强的私有对齐性能

Conclusion: 该框架为人类反馈强化学习提供了有效的差分隐私解决方案，在不同隐私预算下均表现出优于现有方法的对齐性能

Abstract: Preference-based fine-tuning has become an important component in training large language models, and the data used at this stage may contain sensitive user information. A central question is how to design a differentially private pipeline that is well suited to the distinct structure of reinforcement learning from human feedback. We propose a privacy-preserving framework that imposes differential privacy only on reward learning and derives the final policy from the resulting private reward model. Theoretically, we study the suboptimality gap and show that privacy contributes an additional additive term beyond the usual non-private statistical error. We also establish a minimax lower bound and show that the dominant term changes with sample size and privacy level, which in turn characterizes regimes in which the upper bound is rate-optimal up to logarithmic factors. Empirically, synthetic experiments confirm the scaling predicted by the theory, and experiments on the Anthropic HH-RLHF dataset using the Gemma-2B-IT model show stronger private alignment performance than existing differentially private baseline methods across privacy budgets.

</details>


### [161] [Overfitting and Generalizing with (PAC) Bayesian Prediction in Noisy Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22644)
*Xiaohan Zhu,Mesrob I. Ohannessian,Nathan Srebro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于二分类的PAC-Bayes类型学习规则，通过平衡随机"后验"预测器的训练误差与其相对于预定义"先验"的KL散度来扩展MDL方法，并分析了不同正则化参数λ对预测性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将两阶段编码最小描述长度(MDL)学习规则扩展到连续先验和随机预测，建立与贝叶斯预测的联系，并解决传统贝叶斯预测器在不可知情况下可能存在的过拟合和超额损失问题。

Method: 提出了一种PAC-Bayes类型的学习规则，通过参数λ平衡训练误差和KL散度。当λ=1时恢复经验贝叶斯后验，修改后恢复轮廓后验。研究分析了λ≫1时使用样本依赖先验的效果，并精确刻画了欠正则化和过正则化的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1) λ=1时的贝叶斯预测器在不可知情况下会过拟合并导致非零超额损失；2) λ≫1的选择（使用样本依赖先验）能确保在不可知情况下均匀消失的超额损失；3) 精确刻画了不同λ值下欠正则化的影响，区分了温和与灾难性欠正则化机制。

Conclusion: 该工作将Zhu和Srebro[2025]的离散先验工作扩展到PAC-Bayes类型学习规则，并通过严格的贝叶斯解释扩展到更一般的贝叶斯预测，为平衡正则化参数选择提供了理论指导，特别是在不可知学习场景下。

Abstract: We consider a PAC-Bayes type learning rule for binary classification, balancing the training error of a randomized ''posterior'' predictor with its KL divergence to a pre-specified ''prior''. This can be seen as an extension of a modified two-part-code Minimum Description Length (MDL) learning rule, to continuous priors and randomized predictions. With a balancing parameter of $λ=1$ this learning rule recovers an (empirical) Bayes posterior and a modified variant recovers the profile posterior, linking with standard Bayesian prediction (up to the treatment of the single-parameter noise level). However, from a risk-minimization prediction perspective, this Bayesian predictor overfits and can lead to non-vanishing excess loss in the agnostic case. Instead a choice of $λ\gg 1$, which can be seen as using a sample-size-dependent-prior, ensures uniformly vanishing excess loss even in the agnostic case. We precisely characterize the effect of under-regularizing (and over-regularizing) as a function of the balance parameter $λ$, understanding the regimes in which this under-regularization is tempered or catastrophic. This work extends previous work by Zhu and Srebro [2025] that considered only discrete priors to PAC Bayes type learning rules and, through their rigorous Bayesian interpretation, to Bayesian prediction more generally.

</details>


### [162] [REALITrees: Rashomon Ensemble Active Learning for Interpretable Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22750)
*Simon D. Nguyen,Hayden McTavish,Kentaro Hoffman,Cynthia Rudin,Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Rashomon集成主动学习(REAL)方法，通过枚举Rashomon集合中的所有近似最优模型构建委员会，优于传统的基于随机扰动的查询委员会方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统查询委员会方法依赖随机特征子集或数据盲化来诱导模型分歧，虽然近似了认知不确定性，但牺牲了对合理假设空间的直接表征，需要更系统的方法来构建委员会

Method: 提出Rashomon集成主动学习(REAL)：1) 枚举Rashomon集合中的所有近似最优模型构建委员会；2) 采用PAC-Bayesian框架，使用Gibbs后验根据经验风险加权委员会成员；3) 针对稀疏决策树类别精确枚举该集合

Result: 在合成数据和现有主动学习基准测试中，REAL优于随机集成方法，特别是在中等噪声环境中，通过战略性地利用扩展的模型多样性实现更快的收敛

Conclusion: 通过枚举Rashomon集合构建委员会的方法比传统的随机扰动方法更有效，能够更好地表征假设空间，在主动学习中实现更高效的样本选择

Abstract: Active learning reduces labeling costs by selecting samples that maximize information gain. A dominant framework, Query-by-Committee (QBC), typically relies on perturbation-based diversity by inducing model disagreement through random feature subsetting or data blinding. While this approximates one notion of epistemic uncertainty, it sacrifices direct characterization of the plausible hypothesis space. We propose the complementary approach: Rashomon Ensembled Active Learning (REAL) which constructs a committee by exhaustively enumerating the Rashomon Set of all near-optimal models. To address functional redundancy within this set, we adopt a PAC-Bayesian framework using a Gibbs posterior to weight committee members by their empirical risk. Leveraging recent algorithmic advances, we exactly enumerate this set for the class of sparse decision trees. Across synthetic and established active learning baselines, REAL outperforms randomized ensembles, particularly in moderately noisy environments where it strategically leverages expanded model multiplicity to achieve faster convergence.

</details>


### [163] [Stepwise Variational Inference with Vine Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22959)
*Elisabeth Griesbauer,Leiv Rønneberg,Arnoldo Frigessi,Claudia Czado,Ingrid Hobæk Haff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于藤copula的逐步变分推断方法，通过树状结构逐步建模潜在依赖关系，使用Rényi散度而非KL散度，自动确定模型复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分推断方法要么过于简化（如平均场假设），要么需要预先指定复杂参数。需要一种能自动适应数据依赖结构、参数高效且能灵活插值于简单与复杂模型之间的通用变分推断方法

Method: 结合藤copula与逐步估计策略：1）使用藤copula构建嵌套树结构近似后验分布；2）沿藤结构逐树逐步估计变分参数；3）使用Rényi散度而非KL散度定义证据下界；4）引入直观停止准则自动确定所需树的数量

Result: 该方法在参数上比平均场变分推断更节省，同时性能优于平均场方法。特别在稀疏高斯过程等应用中表现良好，能自动插值于平均场与完全依赖模型之间

Conclusion: 提出的逐步藤copula变分推断是一种通用、参数高效的方法，能自动适应数据依赖结构，无需预先指定复杂度参数，在保持计算效率的同时提供更灵活的依赖建模能力

Abstract: We propose stepwise variational inference (VI) with vine copulas: a universal VI procedure that combines vine copulas with a novel stepwise estimation procedure of the variational parameters. Vine copulas consist of a nested sequence of trees built from copulas, where more complex latent dependence can be modeled with increasing number of trees. We propose to estimate the vine copula approximate posterior in a stepwise fashion, tree by tree along the vine structure. Further, we show that the usual backward Kullback-Leibler divergence cannot recover the correct parameters in the vine copula model, thus the evidence lower bound is defined based on the Rényi divergence. Finally, an intuitive stopping criterion for adding further trees to the vine eliminates the need to pre-define a complexity parameter of the variational distribution, as required for most other approaches. Thus, our method interpolates between mean-field VI (MFVI) and full latent dependence. In many applications, in particular sparse Gaussian processes, our method is parsimonious with parameters, while outperforming MFVI.

</details>


### [164] [High-Resolution Tensor-Network Fourier Methods for Exponentially Compressed Non-Gaussian Aggregate Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23106)
*Juan José Rodríguez-Aldavero,Juan José García-Ripoll*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 加权独立随机变量和的特征函数在量化张量链（QTT）表示中具有低秩结构，可实现概率分布的高效压缩，支持金融风险度量的快速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法处理高维概率分布时面临计算复杂度和内存需求爆炸的问题，特别是在金融风险度量（如VaR和ES）等应用中需要高效处理非高斯分布。

Method: 利用量化张量链（QTT）/矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示特征函数的低秩结构，分析变量独立条件下全局特征函数的因子化特性，研究连续模型中的频谱平滑性和离散模型中的能量集中现象。

Result: 伯努利随机变量和的特征函数在D≳300时出现急剧的键维数坍缩，实现多对数时间/内存缩放；对数正态分布可达N=2³⁰频率模式的高分辨率离散化，远超密集实现的N=2²⁴上限。

Conclusion: 特征函数的QTT低秩结构为高效计算非高斯概率分布提供了理论基础，支持金融风险度量的实际应用，展示了张量压缩方法在概率计算中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Characteristic functions of weighted sums of independent random variables exhibit low-rank structure in the quantized tensor train (QTT) representation, also known as matrix product states (MPS), enabling up to exponential compression of their fully non-Gaussian probability distributions. Under variable independence, the global characteristic function factorizes into local terms. Its low-rank QTT structure arises from intrinsic spectral smoothness in continuous models, or from spectral energy concentration as the number of components $D$ grows in discrete models. We demonstrate this on weighted sums of Bernoulli and lognormal random variables. In the former, despite an adversarial, incompressible small-$D$ regime, the characteristic function undergoes a sharp bond-dimension collapse for $D \gtrsim 300$ components, enabling polylogarithmic time and memory scaling. In the latter, the approach reaches high-resolution discretizations of $N = 2^{30}$ frequency modes on standard hardware, far beyond the $N = 2^{24}$ ceiling of dense implementations. These compressed representations enable efficient computation of Value at Risk (VaR) and Expected Shortfall (ES), supporting applications in quantitative finance and beyond.

</details>


### [165] [Between Resolution Collapse and Variance Inflation: Weighted Conformal Anomaly Detection in Low-Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23205)
*Oliver Hennhöfer,Christine Preisach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种连续推理松弛方法，通过连续加权核密度估计将局部适应与尾部解析解耦，解决了加权共形异常检测中p值最小可达性与稳定性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形异常检测在可交换性假设下提供边际有限样本保证，但现实数据常存在分布偏移，需要加权共形方法适应局部非平稳性。这种适应会引发p值最小可达性与稳定性之间的关键权衡：重要性权重局部化到相关校准实例时，有效样本量减少，导致标准共形p值过于保守，而用于缓解此问题的平滑技术会引入条件方差，可能掩盖异常。

Method: 提出连续推理松弛方法，通过连续加权核密度估计将局部适应与尾部解析解耦。该方法将有限样本精确性松弛为渐近有效性，消除蒙特卡洛变异性，并恢复因离散化而损失的统计功效。

Result: 经验评估证实，该方法不仅能在离散基线产生零发现的情况下恢复检测能力，而且在统计功效上优于标准方法，同时在实践中保持有效的边际误差控制。

Conclusion: 连续推理松弛方法解决了加权共形异常检测中的关键权衡问题，通过渐近有效性换取统计功效的提升，在保持有效误差控制的同时显著改善了异常检测能力。

Abstract: Standard conformal anomaly detection provides marginal finite-sample guarantees under the assumption of exchangeability . However, real-world data often exhibit distribution shifts, necessitating a weighted conformal approach to adapt to local non-stationarity. We show that this adaptation induces a critical trade-off between the minimum attainable p-value and its stability. As importance weights localize to relevant calibration instances, the effective sample size decreases. This can render standard conformal p-values overly conservative for effective error control, while the smoothing technique used to mitigate this issue introduces conditional variance, potentially masking anomalies. We propose a continuous inference relaxation that resolves this dilemma by decoupling local adaptation from tail resolution via continuous weighted kernel density estimation. While relaxing finite-sample exactness to asymptotic validity, our method eliminates Monte Carlo variability and recovers the statistical power lost to discretization. Empirical evaluations confirm that our approach not only restores detection capabilities where discrete baselines yield zero discoveries, but outperforms standard methods in statistical power while maintaining valid marginal error control in practice.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Gaussian mixtures and non-parametric likelihoods through the lens of statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23196)
*Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Adityanand Guntuboyina,Satyaki Mukherjee,Hoang-Son Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文从统计力学角度研究高斯混合模型和非参数最大似然估计，建立了超越现有技术的稳定性保证，获得了NPMLE与真实密度之间KL散度的高概率上界，并建立了NPMLE稳定性与统计力学中混沌和多谷现象的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从统计力学视角研究高斯混合模型和非参数最大似然估计问题，旨在建立超越现有技术的稳定性保证，特别是获得NPMLE估计量与真实分布之间KL散度的理论保证，这在文献中已知具有挑战性。

Method: 采用统计力学方法分析NPMLE问题，核心是分析高斯混合密度对数函数类的复杂性，能够处理其无界性。建立了NPMLE稳定性与统计力学中随机能量景观的混沌和多谷现象之间的对应关系。

Result: 获得了NPMLE与真实密度之间KL散度的高概率上界，阶数为min{(log n)^(d+2)/n, log n/√n}，覆盖了n和d相对大小的广泛场景。对NPMLE近似解也获得了类似保证，解决了优化算法在有限时间内停止的现实情况。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了NPMLE的强稳定性保证，超越了现有技术水平。建立的NPMLE稳定性与统计力学混沌现象之间的对应关系，可能对统计和机器学习中的各种随机优化问题具有广泛适用性，特别是与集中现象和Langevin动力学的技术成分的联系。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate Gaussian Mixture Models ({\it abbrv} GMM) and the related problem of non parametric maximum likelihood estimation ({\it abbrv} NPMLE) from the perspective of statistical mechanics. In particular, we establish stability guarantees for the NPMLE procedure that extend well beyond the state of the art. Crucially, we obtain guarantees on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between NPMLE estimators and the ground truth, a type of result which has been known to be challenging in the literature on this problem.
  In particular, we provide high probability upper bounds on the KL divergence between the NPMLE and the true density that are of the order of $\min\big\{\frac{(\log n)^{d+2}}{n} , \frac{\log n}{\sqrt n}\big\}$, which cover a wide range of scenarios for the comparative sizes of $n$ and $d$. We obtain similar guarantees for approximate solutions to the NPMLE problem, addressing realistic situations wherein optimization algorithms need to be stopped in finite time, allowing access only to approximations to the true NPMLE. A technical cornerstone of our approach is an analysis of the function class complexity of logarithms of gaussian mixture densities, which is able to handle their unboundedness, and could be of wider interest.
  We also establish correspondences between stability phenomena in the NPMLE problem and concepts from chaos and multiple valleys in random energy landscapes of statistical mechanics models. We believe that these correspondences may be useful for a wide variety of random optimization problems in statistics and machine learning, especially the connections to the the technical ingredients of concentration phenomena and Langevin dynamics for these models.

</details>


### [167] [A Theory of Nonparametric Covariance Function Estimation for Discretely Observed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23302)
*Yoshikazu Terada,Atsutomo Yara*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了带噪声离散观测的函数型数据在d维域上的非参数协方差函数估计问题，建立了学习型估计器的oracle不等式，推导了深度学习估计器的收敛速率，揭示了协方差函数估计中特有的自适应性与方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离散观测数据估计协方差函数是一个具有挑战性的非参数问题，特别是在多维设置中，协方差函数定义在乘积域上，因此受到维度诅咒的影响。这促使使用自适应估计器（如深度学习估计器），但现有理论结果主要限于具有显式解析表示的估计器，基于学习的估计器性质仍知之甚少。

Method: 建立了一个适用于稀疏和密集观测机制的广泛学习型估计器类的oracle不等式；推导了深度学习估计器在多个协方差函数类上的收敛速率；将学习型估计器的收敛速率与几种现有程序进行比较。

Result: 对于一维光滑类，深度学习估计器是次优的，而局部线性平滑估计器达到更快的速率；对于结构化函数类，深度学习估计器达到最小最大速率（至多相差多对数因子），而局部线性平滑估计器是次优的。这些结果表明协方差函数估计中存在独特的自适应性与方差权衡。

Conclusion: 结构自适应可以缓解维度诅咒，类似于经典的非参数回归。学习型估计器与经典估计器在不同函数类上表现出不同的性能优势，揭示了协方差函数估计中特有的自适应性与方差权衡。

Abstract: We study nonparametric covariance function estimation for functional data observed with noise at discrete locations on a $d$-dimensional domain. Estimating the covariance function from discretely observed data is a challenging nonparametric problem, particularly in multidimensional settings, since the covariance function is defined on a product domain and thus suffers from the curse of dimensionality. This motivates the use of adaptive estimators, such as deep learning estimators. However, existing theoretical results are largely limited to estimators with explicit analytic representations, and the properties of general learning-based estimators remain poorly understood. We establish an oracle inequality for a broad class of learning-based estimators that applies to both sparse and dense observation regimes in a unified manner, and derive convergence rates for deep learning estimators over several classes of covariance functions. The resulting rates suggest that structural adaptation can mitigate the curse of dimensionality, similarly to classical nonparametric regression. We further compare the convergence rates of learning-based estimators with several existing procedures. For a one-dimensional smoothness class, deep learning estimators are suboptimal, whereas local linear smoothing estimators achieve a faster rate. For a structured function class, however, deep learning estimators attain the minimax rate up to polylogarithmic factors, whereas local linear smoothing estimators are suboptimal. These results reveal a distinctive adaptivity-variance trade-off in covariance function estimation.

</details>


### [168] [A Martingale Approach To Fluctuations of Rank Estimators in Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22611)
*Reda Chhaibi,Fabrice Gamboa,Clément Pellegrini*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文使用鞅方法证明了敏感性指数（包括Sobol'指数和Cramér-von Mises指数）估计量的尖锐波动结果，提供了统一的渐近方差结构分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏感性指数（如Sobol'指数和CvM指数）用于量化输入变量对输出变量的非线性影响，已有基于秩的一致估计量。然而，这些估计量的渐近波动特性需要更深入的理论分析，特别是渐近方差的结构尚未完全明确。

Method: 采用鞅方法建立统一的数学框架，处理单变量Sobol'指数、涉及同一标量输入的多个函数的多元扩展，以及CvM指数。在最小正则性假设下工作，推导估计量的渐近分布。

Result: 证明了尖锐的波动结果：对于CvM指数，首次获得了显式的渐近方差公式；对于Sobol'指数，推导了具有更结构化形式的新表达式。统一并简化了先前多个研究的结果。

Conclusion: 鞅方法为敏感性指数的渐近分析提供了统一框架，在最小假设下获得了尖锐的波动结果，特别是为CvM指数提供了首个显式渐近方差公式，深化了对这些重要统计量估计特性的理解。

Abstract: Given a bivariate random pair $(X,Y)$, a natural problem is to estimate, from a single sample $(X_i,Y_i)_{1\le i\le n}$, quantities such as $\mathbb{E}\left[ \mathbb{E}[ Y\mid X ]^2 \right]$. More broadly, sensitivity indices are designed to quantify the possibly nonlinear influence of an input variable $X$ on an output variable $Y$. A classical example is the Sobol' index $$ \frac{\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{E}[Y\mid X])}{\mathrm{Var}(Y)} \in [0,1] \ . $$
  Another important example is the Cramér--von Mises (CvM) index. Following the pioneering work of Chatterjee \cite{chatterjee2021new}, consistent rank-based estimators are now available for such quantities.
  In this paper, we prove sharp fluctuation results using martingale methods. Our framework yields a unified treatment of the univariate Sobol' index, a multivariate extension involving several functions of the same scalar input, and the CvM index. As a consequence, we recover, unify, and simplify results from Gamboa et al. \cite{gamboa2022global, gamboa2023erratum}, Lin--Han \cite{lin2022limit}, and Kroll \cite{kroll2024asymptotic}. In particular, we work under minimal regularity assumptions. Furthermore, while the Gaussian fluctuation phenomenon itself was already known, the novelty lies in the structure of the asymptotic variance: for the CvM index, we obtain, to the best of our knowledge, the first explicit formula, while for the Sobol' index, we derive a new expression with a more structured form.

</details>


### [169] [Fixed-level calibration of the Cauchy combination test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22668)
*Hirofumi Ota*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究Cauchy组合检验(CCT)在固定显著性水平下，当组合p值数量K增长且存在相关性时的渐近性质，提出边界层校准CCT(BL-CCT)来修正参考分布


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCT虽然广泛使用且在小α下渐近有效，但在固定显著性水平下，当K增长且p值存在相关性时，标准Cauchy截断是否准确尚不清楚。需要研究CCT在固定水平下的渐近行为，特别是在相关结构下的表现

Method: 在规范的单因子等相关系数高斯copula模型下，分析原始CCT的渐近性质。发现当相关性固定时，统计量收敛到随机潜因子极限；当相关性随K减弱时，固定水平行为由边界层尺度s_K=√ρ_K(log K)^{3/2}决定。提出边界层校准CCT(BL-CCT)，用单参数高斯平滑Cauchy族替换标准Cauchy参考分布，保持检验统计量不变

Result: 原始CCT在固定α下通常不是渐近精确的：固定正相关时无通用固定水平参考分布；相关性减弱时，原始CCT渐近精确当且仅当ρ_K(log K)^3→0。BL-CCT在更弱的条件ρ_K log K→0下渐近精确，并在有界边界层上提供有用的有限K近似

Conclusion: CCT在固定水平下的尺寸失真源于参考分布而非统计量本身，可通过参考分布修正而不改变检验统计量。BL-CCT通过边界层校准显著改善了CCT在相关p值组合中的表现，为实际应用提供了更可靠的检验方法

Abstract: The Cauchy combination test (CCT) is widely used because it gives a closed-form combined $p$-value and is known to be asymptotically valid as the nominal level $α\downarrow0$ under broad dependence structures. We study a different asymptotic question: whether the usual Cauchy cutoff remains accurate at an ordinary fixed level when the number $K$ of combined $p$-values grows under dependence. Under a canonical one-factor equicorrelated Gaussian copula model, we show that the raw CCT is generally not asymptotically exact at fixed $α$. With fixed positive correlation, the statistic converges to a random latent-factor limit, so there is no universal fixed-level reference law. When the common correlation $ρ_K$ weakens with $K$, fixed-level behaviour is governed by the boundary-layer scale $s_K=\sqrt{ρ_K}(\log K)^{3/2}$, and the raw CCT is asymptotically exact if and only if $ρ_K(\log K)^3\to0$. Because the size distortion arises entirely from the reference law and not from the statistic, it can be corrected without modifying the test statistic itself. We propose the boundary-layer calibrated CCT (BL-CCT), which replaces the standard Cauchy reference by a one-parameter Gaussian-smoothed Cauchy family while keeping the statistic unchanged. This reference-law correction is fundamentally different from existing approaches that modify the test statistic. BL-CCT is asymptotically exact under the weaker condition $ρ_K\log K\to0$ and provides a useful finite-$K$ approximation on bounded boundary layers. Numerical experiments support the theory.

</details>


### [170] [Efficient partially replicated block designs with each replication number one or two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22712)
*R. A. Bailey,Rahul Mukerjee*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究在A-和MV准则下，当每个处理因资源限制只能有1或2次重复时（如早期品种试验）的区组设计。关键创新是不对重复两次的处理子设计施加固定区组大小限制，增加了设计灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究资源限制下（每个处理只能重复1-2次）的区组设计优化问题，这在早期品种试验等场景中常见。传统方法对重复两次处理的子设计有固定区组大小限制，限制了设计灵活性。

Method: 推导所有处理同等重要时的设计独立、尖锐的A-和MV准则下界；探索链接区组设计、在线目录designtheory.org中的设计、部分平衡不完全区组设计及其对偶在本文设定下的应用。

Result: 获得了更广泛设计类别下的A-和MV准则下界，找到了高效设计；展示了链接区组设计、在线目录设计、部分平衡不完全区组设计及其对偶在本文设定下的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过放宽对重复两次处理子设计的固定区组大小限制，扩展了部分重复设计的类别，提高了设计灵活性，为资源限制下的试验设计提供了更优化的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate block designs, under the A- and MV-criteria, when each treatment can have only one or two replications due to resource constraints, as can happen, for example, in early generation varietal trials. While these are commonly known as partially replicated designs, a key new feature of the present work is that no restriction about a constant block size is imposed on the subdesign consisting of the twice replicated treatments. This makes the derivation more challenging but allows us to entertain a wider class of competing designs and hence increases the flexibility of the results. Considering all treatments as equally important, design-independent, sharp lower bounds on the A- and MV-criteria are derived, so as to find highly efficient designs over this wider class. The roles of (a) linked block designs, (b) designs in an online catalog designtheory.org, and (c) partially balanced incomplete block designs, or duals thereof, as adapted to our setup, are explored at length. Illustrative examples are presented.

</details>


### [171] [Critical LAN and Score Tests for Mixed Fractional Models under High-Frequency Observation at H=3/4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22888)
*Chunhao Cai,Yiwu Shang,Cong Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了混合分数布朗运动和混合分数Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程在高频观测下临界边界H=3/4的统计推断问题，建立了临界情况下的中心极限定理和局部渐近正态性，并提出了用于检测H>3/4的得分型检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合分数模型在高频观测下临界边界H=3/4的统计推断问题。当H>3/4时的超临界归一化和H<3/4时的亚临界归一化在该临界值处均失效，需要建立新的理论框架。此外，从数理金融的检验视角出发，需要开发能够检测系统是否进入超临界区域H>3/4的统计检验方法。

Method: 1. 识别精确的临界一阶尺度，证明相应的得分中心极限定理；2. 移除H-得分中的显式线性项后，证明得到的(σ,H)-块已非退化；3. 通过单一三角局部重参数化解决临界点问题；4. 基于此简化推导临界二阶似然展开，为两个模型建立具有完全显式信息常数的局部渐近正态性(LAN)；5. 提出得分型检验方法，使用由LAN漂移决定的右尾拒绝域，并在临界约束H=3/4下进行校准。

Result: 1. 成功识别了临界边界H=3/4处的精确一阶尺度；2. 证明了相应的得分中心极限定理；3. 建立了两个混合分数模型的局部渐近正态性，并获得了完全显式的信息常数；4. 开发了用于检测H>3/4的得分型检验方法，该方法在临界约束下校准，具有完全显式的一阶归一化。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了混合分数模型在临界边界H=3/4处的统计推断难题。与H>3/4区域不同，临界点可以通过单一三角局部重参数化解决。建立的LAN理论和得分型检验为高频金融数据中分数参数的统计推断提供了理论工具，特别适用于检测系统是否进入超临界状态。

Abstract: We study the critical boundary $H=3/4$ for two mixed fractional models under high-frequency observation, namely mixed fractional Brownian motion and mixed fractional Ornstein--Uhlenbeck. For different reasons, both the supercritical normalization for $H>3/4$ and the subcritical normalization for $H<3/4$ fail at this value. We identify the exact critical first-order scales, prove the corresponding score central limit theorems, and show that once the explicit linear term in the $H$-score is removed, the resulting $(σ,H)$-block is already non-degenerate. Thus, in contrast with the regime $H>3/4$, the critical point is resolved by a single triangular local reparametrization. Building on this reduction, we derive the critical second-order likelihood expansion and establish LAN for both models with fully explicit leading information constants.
  Motivated by a testing viewpoint from mathematical finance, we additionally derive score-type tests at the critical boundary. In particular, we formulate one-sided procedures for detecting whether the system enters the supercritical side $H>3/4$. The proposed tests are calibrated under the critical constraint $H=3/4$, use a right-tail rejection region dictated by the LAN drift, and admit fully explicit normalizations at the leading order.

</details>


### [172] [Asymptotics of Nonparametric Estimation under general non-monotone MAR missingness: A Bayesian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23449)
*Badr-Eddine Chérief-Abdellatif,Jeffrey Näf*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文将非参数贝叶斯理论扩展到非单调随机缺失(MAR)设置，提出了后验收缩定理，证明了在MAR下可以以极小极大后验收缩率估计未污染数据的密度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失值在数据科学中普遍存在，对统计分析有潜在危害。尽管已有许多方法和理论结果，但在一般非单调随机缺失(MAR)情况下仍有许多开放问题，特别是如何在这种设置下进行非参数贝叶斯推断

Method: 扩展非参数贝叶斯理论到MAR设置，引入后验收缩定理，在MAR和附加的温和正性条件下证明理论结果，开发能够从缺失值污染数据中返回未污染分布一致性估计样本的算法

Result: 证明了在MAR下，尽管存在缺失值，未污染数据的密度可以以极小极大后验收缩率（达到对数因子）进行估计，这是第一个在Rubin的MAR定义下证明未污染分布可以一致性估计的非参数结果

Conclusion: 该工作为非参数贝叶斯理论在MAR设置下的扩展提供了理论基础，开发了能够从缺失值污染数据中恢复未污染分布的一致性估计算法，填补了该领域的重要理论空白

Abstract: Missing values are ubiquitous in (data) science, with potential detrimental consequences for any statistical analysis. As a consequence, a wealth of methods and theoretical results have been developed in recent years. Still, many questions remain open, in particular in the case of general non-monotone missing at random (MAR). In this work, we extend nonparametric Bayesian theory to this MAR setting. We introduce a general theorem of posterior contraction under MAR and an additional mild positivity condition. Using this result, we are able to show that, despite the missing values, the density of the uncontaminated data can be estimated with the minimax posterior contraction rate up to log factors. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first nonparametric result showing that the uncontaminated distribution can be consistently estimated under Rubin's MAR definition. As a consequence, we obtain an algorithm that takes data contaminated with missing values and returns a sample from a provably consistent estimate of the uncontaminated distribution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Bridging the Gap Between Climate Science and Machine Learning in Climate Model Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22320)
*Luca Schmidt,Nina Effenberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个整合气候科学与机器学习视角的框架，通过设计易于采用且针对明确任务的模拟器，并证明其可靠性，来克服机器学习气候模拟器应用中的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候模型对气候决策至关重要，但计算和技术需求高。机器学习模拟器虽能降低计算成本，但实际应用中面临多重障碍：可访问性有限、缺乏专业知识、以及对物理基础不足的机器学习方法普遍不信任。

Method: 引入一个整合气候科学与机器学习视角的框架，设计易于采用的模拟器，这些模拟器针对明确的任务，并能够证明其可靠性。

Result: 研究发现，设计易于采用、针对明确任务且能证明可靠性的模拟器，为弥合气候科学与机器学习领域之间的差距提供了有前景的路径。

Conclusion: 通过整合气候科学与机器学习视角，并专注于开发易于采用、任务明确且可靠的模拟器，可以有效克服当前机器学习气候模拟器应用中的障碍，促进两个领域的融合。

Abstract: While climate models provide insights for climate decision-making, their use is constrained by significant computational and technical demands. Although machine learning (ML) emulators offer a way to bypass the high computational costs, their effective use remains challenging. The hurdles are diverse, ranging from limited accessibility and a lack of specialized knowledge to a general mistrust of ML methods that are perceived as insufficiently physical. Here, we introduce a framework to overcome these barriers by integrating both climate science and machine learning perspectives. We find that designing easy-to-adopt emulators that address a clearly defined task and demonstrating their reliability offers a promising path for bridging the gap between our two fields.

</details>


### [174] [Problems with Chinchilla Approach 2: Systematic Biases in IsoFLOP Parabola Fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22339)
*Eric Czech,Zhiwei Xu,Yael Elmatad,Yixin Wang,William Held*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chinchilla Approach 2在拟合神经缩放定律时存在系统性偏差，导致计算最优分配估计不准确。Chinchilla Approach 3能消除这些偏差，但常被认为数据效率低、数值不稳定。论文证明这些担忧不成立，并提出利用变量投影的改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chinchilla Approach 2作为广泛使用的神经缩放定律拟合方法，其抛物线近似在计算最优分配估计中引入系统性偏差。这些偏差在实际应用中导致显著的计算资源浪费和经济成本，特别是在大规模模型训练中。论文旨在揭示这些偏差的来源，并改进Chinchilla Approach 3以提供更准确的估计方法。

Method: 论文分析了Chinchilla Approach 2偏差的三个来源：IsoFLOP采样网格宽度（泰勒近似精度）、未居中的IsoFLOP采样、以及损失表面不对称性（α≠β）。针对Chinchilla Approach 3常被认为数据效率低、数值不稳定等问题，论文提出利用变量投影方法，通过利用目标函数的局部线性结构，将五维参数推断简化为二维优化问题，实现良好条件、解析可微且适合密集网格搜索的解决方案。

Result: 应用Chinchilla Approach 2到Llama 3 IsoFLOP数据时，偏差导致参数分配不足，相当于38×10²⁵ FLOP训练预算的6.5%，以及在50% H100 MFU下140万美元（90%置信区间：41.2万-290万美元）的不必要计算成本。多模态模型的模拟显示更大的机会成本。改进的Chinchilla Approach 3方法能够消除这些偏差，提供无偏的参数推断。

Conclusion: Chinchilla Approach 2的系统性偏差在实际应用中导致显著的计算资源浪费。Chinchilla Approach 3经过适当改进（特别是利用变量投影）可以消除这些偏差，同时保持数值稳定性和计算效率。该方法可以作为Approach 2的替代方案，或作为更丰富缩放定律公式的扩展基础。

Abstract: Chinchilla Approach 2 is among the most widely used methods for fitting neural scaling laws. Its parabolic approximation introduces systematic biases in compute-optimal allocation estimates, even on noise-free synthetic data. Applied to published Llama 3 IsoFLOP data at open frontier compute scales, these biases imply a parameter underallocation corresponding to 6.5% of the $3.8\times10^{25}$ FLOP training budget and \$1.4M (90% CI: \$412K-\$2.9M) in unnecessary compute at 50% H100 MFU. Simulated multimodal model misallocations show even greater opportunity costs due to higher loss surface asymmetry. Three sources of this error are examined: IsoFLOP sampling grid width (Taylor approximation accuracy), uncentered IsoFLOP sampling, and loss surface asymmetry ($α\neq β$). Chinchilla Approach 3 largely eliminates these biases but is often regarded as less data-efficient, numerically unstable, prone to local minima, and harder to implement. Each concern is shown to be unfounded or addressable, especially when the partially linear structure of the objective is exploited via Variable Projection, enabling unbiased inference on all five loss surface parameters through a two-dimensional optimization that is well-conditioned, analytically differentiable, and amenable to dense, or even exhaustive, grid search. It may serve as a more convenient replacement for Approach 2 or a more scalable alternative for adaptations of Approach 3 to richer scaling law formulations.

</details>


### [175] [A Theoretical Framework for Energy-Aware Gradient Pruning in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22465)
*Emmanouil M. Athanasakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出成本加权幅度剪枝(CWMP)方法，在联邦学习中考虑硬件能耗差异进行梯度稀疏化，相比传统Top-K方法建立更优的性能-能耗帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习中的梯度稀疏化方法（如Top-K幅度剪枝）仅考虑梯度幅度，忽略了硬件层面的能耗差异。实际中不同参数更新的传输和内存更新成本不同，需要设计能耗感知的稀疏化策略。

Method: 将剪枝过程形式化为能量约束的投影问题，考虑后向传播阶段内存密集型与计算高效操作之间的硬件差异。提出成本加权幅度剪枝(CWMP)选择规则，根据参数更新的幅度相对于其物理成本进行优先级排序。

Result: 证明CWMP是该约束投影问题的最优贪心解，并提供其全局能量效率的概率分析。在非独立同分布CIFAR-10基准测试中，CWMP相比Top-K基线始终建立更优的性能-能耗帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: CWMP通过考虑硬件能耗差异的梯度稀疏化，在联邦学习中实现了更优的性能-能耗权衡，为边缘设备上的高效联邦学习提供了理论保证和实践方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is constrained by the communication and energy limitations of decentralized edge devices. While gradient sparsification via Top-K magnitude pruning effectively reduces the communication payload, it remains inherently energy-agnostic. It assumes all parameter updates incur identical downstream transmission and memory-update costs, ignoring hardware realities. We formalize the pruning process as an energy-constrained projection problem that accounts for the hardware-level disparities between memory-intensive and compute-efficient operations during the post-backpropagation phase. We propose Cost-Weighted Magnitude Pruning (CWMP), a selection rule that prioritizes parameter updates based on their magnitude relative to their physical cost. We demonstrate that CWMP is the optimal greedy solution to this constrained projection and provide a probabilistic analysis of its global energy efficiency. Numerical results on a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark show that CWMP consistently establishes a superior performance-energy Pareto frontier compared to the Top-K baseline.

</details>


### [176] [Towards The Implicit Bias on Multiclass Separable Data Under Norm Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22824)
*Shengping Xie,Zekun Wu,Quan Chen,Kaixu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NucGD优化器，通过核范数约束在可分离多类数据上强制低秩结构，分析优化几何对隐式偏差的影响，并开发可扩展的SVD-free更新规则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度算法在过参数化模型中诱导的隐式偏差机制，特别是优化几何如何影响多类可分离数据上的解，需要一种能够强制低秩结构的几何感知优化器

Method: 基于归一化最陡下降(NSD)框架，提出NucGD优化器，通过核范数约束强制低秩结构；连接低秩投影方法提供统一视角；开发基于异步幂迭代的高效SVD-free更新规则；实证分析随机优化动态（小批量采样和动量）对收敛到最大边际解的影响

Result: NucGD能够有效强制低秩结构，与低秩投影方法建立理论联系；SVD-free更新规则实现可扩展训练；实证表明梯度噪声水平（小批量大小和动量）调节收敛到期望最大边际解的过程

Conclusion: 优化几何在多类可分离数据上塑造隐式偏差中起关键作用，NucGD提供了一种几何感知的方法来强制低秩结构，随机优化动态显著影响收敛行为，为理解过参数化模型的泛化提供了新视角

Abstract: Implicit bias induced by gradient-based algorithms is essential to the generalization of overparameterized models, yet its mechanisms can be subtle. This work leverages the Normalized Steepest Descent} (NSD) framework to investigate how optimization geometry shapes solutions on multiclass separable data. We introduce NucGD, a geometry-aware optimizer designed to enforce low rank structures through nuclear norm constraints. Beyond the algorithm itself, we connect NucGD with emerging low-rank projection methods, providing a unified perspective. To enable scalable training, we derive an efficient SVD-free update rule via asynchronous power iteration. Furthermore, we empirically dissect the impact of stochastic optimization dynamics, characterizing how varying levels of gradient noise induced by mini-batch sampling and momentum modulate the convergence toward the expected maximum margin solutions.Our code is accessible at: https://github.com/Tsokarsic/observing-the-implicit-bias-on-multiclass-seperable-data.

</details>


### [177] [General Machine Learning: Theory for Learning Under Variable Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23220)
*Aomar Osmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个处理学习过程中学习者、记忆状态和评估条件随时间变化的制度变化学习框架，建立了核心学习理论对象并证明了初步定理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究学习过程中的制度变化问题，即学习者、记忆状态和评估条件可能随时间演变的情况。需要为这种设置定义核心的学习理论对象并建立支持定理的基础结构。

Method: 开发了一个以可容许传输、受保护核心保持和评估者感知学习演化为中心的制度变化框架。建立了可容许性的闭包结果，为真正多制度设置中的忠实固定本体论约简开发了结构阻碍论证，引入了受保护稳定性模板，并在受控子类（包括凸和演绎设置）上提供了显式数值和符号见证。

Result: 建立了评估者因子分解、态射、复合和语义可通约层间的部分核级对齐的定理层结果。通过一个两制度工作示例，在受控子类上明确了可容许性证书、受保护评估核心和制度变化成本。证明了第一个核级兼容性结果和受控单调演绎见证。

Conclusion: 该论文为制度变化学习引入了一个结构化的学习理论框架及其第一个定理支持层，但并非所有学习系统的完整定量理论。这是一个基础性和结构性的贡献，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study learning under regime variation, where the learner, its memory state, and the evaluative conditions may evolve over time. This paper is a foundational and structural contribution: its goal is to define the core learning-theoretic objects required for such settings and to establish their first theorem-supporting consequences.
  The paper develops a regime-varying framework centered on admissible transport, protected-core preservation, and evaluator-aware learning evolution. It records the immediate closure consequences of admissibility, develops a structural obstruction argument for faithful fixed-ontology reduction in genuinely multi-regime settings, and introduces a protected-stability template together with explicit numerical and symbolic witnesses on controlled subclasses, including convex and deductive settings. It also establishes theorem-layer results on evaluator factorization, morphisms, composition, and partial kernel-level alignment across semantically commensurable layers.
  A worked two-regime example makes the admissibility certificate, protected evaluative core, and regime-variation cost explicit on a controlled subclass. The symbolic component is deliberately restricted in scope: the paper establishes a first kernel-level compatibility result together with a controlled monotonic deductive witness. The manuscript should therefore be read as introducing a structured learning-theoretic framework for regime-varying learning together with its first theorem-supporting layer, not as a complete quantitative theory of all learning systems.

</details>


### [178] [Robustness Quantification for Discriminative Models: a New Robustness Metric and its Application to Dynamic Classifier Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23318)
*Rodrigo F. L. Lassance,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出适用于任何概率判别分类器和特征类型的新鲁棒性度量，用于评估预测可靠性并开发动态分类器选择策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性量化方法应用受限，需要生成模型、特定架构或离散特征，需要更通用的可靠性评估方法

Method: 提出新的鲁棒性度量，适用于任何概率判别分类器和特征类型，基于分类器能承受多少不确定性而不改变预测

Result: 新度量能区分可靠和不可靠预测，并基于此开发动态分类器选择策略

Conclusion: 提出的通用鲁棒性度量扩展了可靠性评估的应用范围，为动态分类器选择提供了新方法

Abstract: Among the different possible strategies for evaluating the reliability of individual predictions of classifiers, robustness quantification stands out as a method that evaluates how much uncertainty a classifier could cope with before changing its prediction. However, its applicability is more limited than some of its alternatives, since it requires the use of generative models and restricts the analyses either to specific model architectures or discrete features. In this work, we propose a new robustness metric applicable to any probabilistic discriminative classifier and any type of features. We demonstrate that this new metric is capable of distinguishing between reliable and unreliable predictions, and use this observation to develop new strategies for dynamic classifier selection.

</details>


### [179] [Graph Energy Matching: Transport-Aligned Energy-Based Modeling for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23398)
*Michal Balcerak,Suprosana Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Michael S. Albergo,Yilun Du,Bjoern Menze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM是一种用于图生成的基于能量的模型框架，通过结合传输对齐的引导和能量优化，在离散图域中实现了与扩散模型相媲美的采样质量，同时支持组合生成和约束采样等推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散能量模型在高效高质量采样方面存在困难，因为离支撑区域常包含虚假局部极小值，导致采样器陷入困境和训练不稳定，这造成了与离散扩散模型之间的保真度差距。需要一种能够同时提供传输引导和细化采样的图生成框架。

Method: GEM基于Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto方案的传输映射优化视角，学习一个置换不变势能函数，该函数同时提供从噪声到数据的传输对齐引导，并在高数据似然区域细化样本。引入基于能量的切换采样协议，无缝桥接：(i)快速梯度引导传输到高概率区域和(ii)混合机制以探索学习的图分布。

Result: 在分子图基准测试中，GEM匹配或超越了强大的离散扩散基线模型。除了样本质量外，显式相对似然建模支持推理时的目标探索，促进了组合生成、属性约束采样和图之间的测地线插值。

Conclusion: GEM框架成功弥合了离散能量模型与扩散模型之间的保真度差距，通过结合传输对齐引导和能量优化实现了高质量的图生成，同时保留了能量模型在组合推理和约束采样方面的优势。

Abstract: Energy-based models for discrete domains, such as graphs, explicitly capture relative likelihoods, naturally enabling composable probabilistic inference tasks like conditional generation or enforcing constraints at test-time. However, discrete energy-based models typically struggle with efficient and high-quality sampling, as off-support regions often contain spurious local minima, trapping samplers and causing training instabilities. This has historically resulted in a fidelity gap relative to discrete diffusion models. We introduce Graph Energy Matching (GEM), a generative framework for graphs that closes this fidelity gap. Motivated by the transport map optimization perspective of the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme, GEM learns a permutation-invariant potential energy that simultaneously provides transport-aligned guidance from noise toward data and refines samples within regions of high data likelihood. Further, we introduce a sampling protocol that leverages an energy-based switch to seamlessly bridge: (i) rapid, gradient-guided transport toward high-probability regions to (ii) a mixing regime for exploration of the learned graph distribution. On molecular graph benchmarks, GEM matches or exceeds strong discrete diffusion baselines. Beyond sample quality, explicit modeling of relative likelihood enables targeted exploration at inference time, facilitating compositional generation, property-constrained sampling, and geodesic interpolation between graphs.

</details>


### [180] [Beyond Hard Constraints: Budget-Conditioned Reachability For Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22292)
*Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Akshat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于安全可达性分析的离线安全强化学习方法，通过定义安全条件可达集解耦奖励最大化和累积安全成本约束，避免不稳定的min/max对抗优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界任务需要在奖励最大化和安全约束之间取得平衡，传统方法常导致不稳定的min/max对抗优化。现有可达性分析方法主要处理硬安全约束，很少扩展到累积成本约束。

Method: 1) 定义安全条件可达集，解耦奖励最大化和累积安全成本约束；2) 利用该集在不使用不稳定min/max或拉格朗日优化的情况下强制执行安全约束；3) 开发从固定数据集学习安全策略的离线安全RL算法。

Result: 在标准离线安全RL基准测试和真实世界海上导航任务中，该方法匹配或优于最先进的基线方法，同时保持安全性。

Conclusion: 提出的安全条件可达集方法为离线安全RL提供了一种有效框架，能够处理累积成本约束，避免传统对抗优化的不稳定性，在实际应用中表现出色。

Abstract: Sequential decision making using Markov Decision Process underpins many realworld applications. Both model-based and model free methods have achieved strong results in these settings. However, real-world tasks must balance reward maximization with safety constraints, often conflicting objectives, that can lead to unstable min/max, adversarial optimization. A promising alternative is safety reachability analysis, which precomputes a forward-invariant safe state, action set, ensuring that an agent starting inside this set remains safe indefinitely. Yet, most reachability based methods address only hard safety constraints, and little work extends reachability to cumulative cost constraints. To address this, first, we define a safetyconditioned reachability set that decouples reward maximization from cumulative safety cost constraints. Second, we show how this set enforces safety constraints without unstable min/max or Lagrangian optimization, yielding a novel offline safe RL algorithm that learns a safe policy from a fixed dataset without environment interaction. Finally, experiments on standard offline safe RL benchmarks, and a real world maritime navigation task demonstrate that our method matches or outperforms state of the art baselines while maintaining safety.

</details>


### [181] [Efficient Embedding-based Synthetic Data Generation for Complex Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22294)
*Srideepika Jayaraman,Achille Fokoue,Dhaval Patel,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于嵌入空间的针对性数据生成管道，通过分析生成数据在嵌入空间的分布密度与预测准确性的相关性，改进合成数据生成的质量和多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成（SDG）利用大语言模型提升小模型性能，但面临确保生成数据质量和多样性的关键挑战。需要解决生成数据在嵌入空间中的分布问题，以优化模型微调效果。

Method: 分析生成数据在嵌入空间的多样性和分布，发现特定邻域内示例密度与从该区域抽取示例的预测准确性存在强相关性。基于此提出针对性的嵌入空间采样管道，增强数据多样性。

Result: 提出的嵌入空间采样方法在多个基准测试中一致提升了性能，验证了嵌入空间数据分布密度与预测准确性相关性的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过分析嵌入空间数据分布与预测性能的关系，提出的针对性采样管道能够有效提升合成数据生成的质量和多样性，为改进小语言模型微调提供了新方法。

Abstract: Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs), has recently been recognized and broadly adopted as an effective approach to improve the performance of smaller but more resource and compute efficient LLMs through fine-tuning. A key challenge in SDG is ensuring the quality and diversity of the generated data. In this paper, we analyze the diversity and distribution of generated data in the embedding space, and demonstrate a strong correlation between the density of examples within a specific neighborhood and the accuracy of predictions on examples drawn from that region. Building on this insight, we present a targeted pipeline for embedding-based sampling that enhances data diversity and consistently improves performance across several benchmarks.

</details>


### [182] [Between the Layers Lies the Truth: Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs Using Intra-Layer Local Information Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22299)
*Zvi N. Badash,Yonatan Belinkov,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于内部表示跨层一致性模式的紧凑型不确定性估计方法，在保持单次前向传播低成本的同时，在分布外和量化场景下优于传统探测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常表现出过度自信的错误预测，需要可靠的不确定性估计。基于输出的启发式方法成本低但脆弱，而探测内部表示的方法有效但维度高且难以迁移。

Method: 提出一种紧凑的、针对每个实例的不确定性估计方法，通过单次前向传播对内部表示中的跨层一致性模式进行评分。

Result: 在三个模型上，该方法在分布内匹配探测性能（AUPRC差异≤-1.8%，Brier分数差异≤+4.9点）。在跨数据集迁移中，始终优于探测方法（AUPRC增益达+2.86，Brier分数增益达+21.02）。在4位权重量化下保持鲁棒性，平均优于探测方法+1.94 AUPRC点和+5.33 Brier点。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种轻量级、紧凑的方式来捕获大语言模型中的可迁移不确定性，同时通过分析层间交互揭示了不同模型编码不确定性的差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often confidently wrong, making reliable uncertainty estimation (UE) essential. Output-based heuristics are cheap but brittle, while probing internal representations is effective yet high-dimensional and hard to transfer.
  We propose a compact, per-instance UE method that scores cross-layer agreement patterns in internal representations using a single forward pass.
  Across three models, our method matches probing in-distribution, with mean diagonal differences of at most $-1.8$ AUPRC percentage points and $+4.9$ Brier score points. Under cross-dataset transfer, it consistently outperforms probing, achieving off-diagonal gains up to $+2.86$ AUPRC and $+21.02$ Brier points. Under 4-bit weight-only quantization, it remains robust, improving over probing by $+1.94$ AUPRC points and $+5.33$ Brier points on average.
  Beyond performance, examining specific layer--layer interactions reveals differences in how disparate models encode uncertainty. Altogether, our UE method offers a lightweight, compact means to capture transferable uncertainty in LLMs.

</details>


### [183] [Scaling Attention via Feature Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22300)
*Yan Xie,Tiansheng Wen,Tangda Huang,Bo Chen,Chenyu You,Stefanie Jegelka,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sparse Feature Attention (SFA) 通过特征稀疏性而非序列稀疏性来降低Transformer注意力计算成本，将复杂度从O(n²d)降至O(n²k²/d)，同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过局部窗口、核近似或token级稀疏性来降低注意力计算成本，但这些方法都会导致精度下降。本文探索了正交的特征稀疏性这一新维度，旨在在保持高维表达能力的同时显著降低计算成本。

Method: 提出Sparse Feature Attention (SFA)，将查询和键表示为k-稀疏编码，保留高维表达能力的同时将注意力成本从Θ(n²d)降至Θ(n²k²/d)。引入FlashSFA，这是一个IO感知的内核，扩展了FlashAttention，可直接在稀疏重叠上操作而无需物化密集分数矩阵。

Result: 在GPT-2和Qwen3预训练中，SFA与密集基线匹配，同时速度提升高达2.5倍，FLOPs和KV缓存减少近50%。在合成和下游基准测试中，SFA在长上下文中保持检索准确性和鲁棒性，优于特征多样性崩溃的短嵌入基线。

Conclusion: 特征级稀疏性是高效注意力的一个互补且未被充分探索的维度，使Transformer能够以最小质量损失扩展到数量级更长的上下文。SFA为超长上下文Transformer扩展提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to ultra-long contexts is bottlenecked by the $O(n^2 d)$ cost of self-attention. Existing methods reduce this cost along the sequence axis through local windows, kernel approximations, or token-level sparsity, but these approaches consistently degrade accuracy. In this paper, we instead explore an orthogonal axis: feature sparsity. We propose Sparse Feature Attention (SFA), where queries and keys are represented as $k$-sparse codes that preserve high-dimensional expressivity while reducing the cost of attention from $Θ(n^2 d)$ to $Θ(n^2 k^2/d)$. To make this efficient at scale, we introduce FlashSFA, an IO-aware kernel that extends FlashAttention to operate directly on sparse overlaps without materializing dense score matrices. Across GPT-2 and Qwen3 pretraining, SFA matches dense baselines while improving speed by up to $2.5\times$ and reducing FLOPs and KV-cache by nearly 50\%. On synthetic and downstream benchmarks, SFA preserves retrieval accuracy and robustness at long contexts, outperforming short-embedding baselines that collapse feature diversity. These results establish feature-level sparsity as a complementary and underexplored axis for efficient attention, enabling Transformers to scale to orders-of-magnitude longer contexts with minimal quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YannX1e/Sparse-Feature-Attention.

</details>


### [184] [Latent Semantic Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22301)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种将LLM隐藏状态解释为潜在语义流形上点的数学框架，使用Fisher信息度量，将token对应为划分流形的Voronoi区域，并定义了表达性间隙来衡量词汇离散化带来的语义失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在连续向量空间中进行内部计算，但输出离散token，这种基本不匹配的几何后果尚未被充分理解。需要建立数学框架来理解LLM隐藏状态的几何结构及其与离散词汇表的关系。

Method: 开发了一个将LLM隐藏状态解释为黎曼子流形（配备Fisher信息度量）上点的数学框架，其中token对应划分流形的Voronoi区域。定义了表达性间隙来衡量语义失真，并证明了两个定理：有限词汇表失真的率失真下界，以及通过余面积公式得到的表达性间隙线性体积缩放定律。在六个transformer架构（124M-1.5B参数）上验证了预测。

Result: 验证了普遍的沙漏形内在维度剖面、平滑曲率结构，以及线性间隙缩放（斜率0.87-1.12，R^2 > 0.985）。边界邻近表示的硬核分布在所有模型中保持稳定，不随规模变化，为困惑度提供了几何分解。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解LLM的几何结构提供了理论基础，揭示了词汇离散化带来的语义失真规律。研究结果对架构设计、模型压缩、解码策略和缩放定律具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform internal computations in continuous vector spaces yet produce discrete tokens -- a fundamental mismatch whose geometric consequences remain poorly understood. We develop a mathematical framework that interprets LLM hidden states as points on a latent semantic manifold: a Riemannian submanifold equipped with the Fisher information metric, where tokens correspond to Voronoi regions partitioning the manifold. We define the expressibility gap, a geometric measure of the semantic distortion from vocabulary discretization, and prove two theorems: a rate-distortion lower bound on distortion for any finite vocabulary, and a linear volume scaling law for the expressibility gap via the coarea formula. We validate these predictions across six transformer architectures (124M-1.5B parameters), confirming universal hourglass intrinsic dimension profiles, smooth curvature structure, and linear gap scaling with slopes 0.87-1.12 (R^2 > 0.985). The margin distribution across models reveals a persistent hard core of boundary-proximal representations invariant to scale, providing a geometric decomposition of perplexity. We discuss implications for architecture design, model compression, decoding strategies, and scaling laws

</details>


### [185] [Research on Individual Trait Clustering and Development Pathway Adaptation Based on the K-means Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22302)
*Qianru Wei,Jihaoyu Yang,Cheng Zhang,Jinming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用K-means聚类算法分析大学生特征数据，为不同特征组合的学生提供精准的职业指导建议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业指导方法多关注职业路径预测，但较少研究不同特征组合学生在特定职业方向的适配性。随着信息技术发展，人工智能和机器学习在教育领域展现出巨大潜力，需要探索如何利用聚类算法为大学生提供精准职业指导

Method: 收集3000多名学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、人格特质和学生干部经历数据，采用K-means聚类算法将学生分为四个主要群体。该算法通过最小化簇内平方误差，将具有相似特征的学生分组，确保同一簇内学生特征高度相似，不同簇间差异最大化

Result: 研究结果显示，具有不同特征组合的学生适合不同的职业方向。基于聚类结果为每个群体提供针对性的职业指导建议，为个性化职业指导提供科学依据，有效提升学生就业成功率

Conclusion: K-means聚类算法能够有效识别学生特征模式，为精准职业指导提供支持。未来研究可通过扩大样本量、增加特征变量和考虑外部因素，进一步提高聚类精度和指导效果

Abstract: With the development of information technology, the application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in the field of education shows great potential. This study aims to explore how to utilize K-means clustering algorithm to provide accurate career guidance for college students. Existing methods mostly focus on the prediction of career paths, but there are fewer studies on the fitness of students with different combinations of characteristics in specific career directions. In this study, we analyze the data of more than 3000 students on their CET-4 scores, GPA, personality traits and student cadre experiences, and use the K-means clustering algorithm to classify the students into four main groups. The K-means clustering algorithm groups students with similar characteristics into one group by minimizing the intra-cluster squared error, ensuring that the students within the same cluster are highly similar in their characteristics, and that differences between different clusters are maximized. Based on the clustering results, targeted career guidance suggestions are provided for each group. The results of the study show that students with different combinations of characteristics are suitable for different career directions, which provides a scientific basis for personalized career guidance and effectively enhances students' employment success rate. Future research can further improve the precision of clustering and the guidance effect by expanding the sample size, increasing the feature variables and considering external factors.

</details>


### [186] [Sample Transform Cost-Based Training-Free Hallucination Detector for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22303)
*Zeyang Ding,Xinglin Hu,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优传输距离的LLM幻觉检测方法，通过计算样本间Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化分布复杂度，无需训练即可检测幻觉


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM幻觉是可信部署的主要障碍，需要准确、轻量且广泛适用的检测器。LLM与提示定义的分布复杂度可作为幻觉指标，但分布密度未知且样本是离散分布，量化复杂度面临挑战

Method: 计算成对样本的token嵌入之间的最优传输距离，得到Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化分布复杂度。基于该矩阵推导两个互补信号：AvgWD（平均成本）和EigenWD（成本复杂度）。通过教师强制将框架扩展到黑盒LLM

Result: 实验表明AvgWD和EigenWD与强不确定性基线竞争，在不同模型和数据集上表现出互补行为，证明分布复杂度是LLM真实性的有效信号

Conclusion: 分布复杂度是检测LLM幻觉的有效指标，提出的基于Wasserstein距离的训练免费检测器在多种场景下表现良好，为可信LLM部署提供了实用工具

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) remain a central obstacle to trustworthy deployment, motivating detectors that are accurate, lightweight, and broadly applicable. Since an LLM with a prompt defines a conditional distribution, we argue that the complexity of the distribution is an indicator of hallucination. However, the density of the distribution is unknown and the samples (i.e., responses generated for the prompt) are discrete distributions, which leads to a significant challenge in quantifying the complexity of the distribution. We propose to compute the optimal-transport distances between the sets of token embeddings of pairwise samples, which yields a Wasserstein distance matrix measuring the costs of transforming between the samples. This Wasserstein distance matrix provides a means to quantify the complexity of the distribution defined by the LLM with the prompt. Based on the Wasserstein distance matrix, we derive two complementary signals: AvgWD, measuring the average cost, and EigenWD, measuring the cost complexity. This leads to a training-free detector for hallucinations in LLMs. We further extend the framework to black-box LLMs via teacher forcing with an accessible teacher model. Experiments show that AvgWD and EigenWD are competitive with strong uncertainty baselines and provide complementary behavior across models and datasets, highlighting distribution complexity as an effective signal for LLM truthfulness.

</details>


### [187] [Mitigating Premature Discretization with Progressive Quantization for Robust Vector Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22304)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProVQ通过渐进量化解决现有VQ方法中的"过早离散化"问题，在图像和生物序列建模中均取得显著性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量量化(VQ)方法存在"过早离散化"的根本冲突：在编码器捕获底层数据流形之前就强制离散化，这限制了模型性能

Method: 提出渐进量化(ProVQ)，将量化硬度动态作为VQ训练中先前被忽视的基本轴，将量化视为从连续潜在空间平滑退火到离散空间的课程

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-100基准上改进了重建和生成性能；在蛋白质结构标记化方面，在StrutTokenBench排行榜上建立了新的性能上限

Conclusion: ProVQ通过解决过早离散化问题，在多种模态上展示了广泛的有效性，为生成建模提供了显著提升

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) has become the cornerstone of tokenization for many multimodal Large Language Models and diffusion synthesis. However, existing VQ paradigms suffer from a fundamental conflict: they enforce discretization before the encoder has captured the underlying data manifold. We term this phenomenon Premature Discretization. To resolve this, we propose Progressive Quantization (ProVQ), which incorporates the dynamics of quantization hardness as a fundamental yet previously overlooked axis in VQ training. By treating quantization as a curriculum that smoothly anneals from a continuous latent space to a discrete one, ProVQ effectively guides the codebook toward the well-expanded manifolds. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the broad effectiveness of ProVQ across diverse modalities. We report improved reconstruction and generative performance on the ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-100 benchmarks, highlighting the ProVQ's boost for generative modeling. Furthermore, ProVQ proves highly effective for modeling complex biological sequences, establishing a new performance ceiling for protein structure tokenization on the StrutTokenBench leaderboard.

</details>


### [188] [UniFluids: Unified Neural Operator Learning with Conditional Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22309)
*Haosen Li,Qi Meng,Jiahao Li,Rui Zhang,Ruihua Song,Liang Ma,Zhi-Ming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniFluids：基于条件流匹配的统一PDE解算子学习框架，利用扩散Transformer实现跨维度、跨物理变量的并行序列生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PDE模拟在科学研究中具有重要意义，深度学习学习PDE解算子具有巨大潜力。现有自回归PDE基础模型存在效率问题，需要一种能够统一处理不同维度、不同物理变量PDE的并行生成方法。

Method: 提出UniFluids框架：1）采用条件流匹配实现并行序列生成；2）引入统一的四维时空表示处理异构PDE数据集；3）利用x-预测方法改进流匹配算子学习；4）基于扩散Transformer架构实现可扩展性。

Result: 在1D、2D、3D空间维度的多个PDE数据集上进行大规模评估，UniFluids展现出强大的预测精度、良好的可扩展性和跨场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFluids是首个采用流匹配实现统一PDE解算子学习的并行生成框架，通过统一表示和x-预测方法显著提升了预测精度，为PDE模拟提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) simulation holds extensive significance in scientific research. Currently, the integration of deep neural networks to learn solution operators of PDEs has introduced great potential. In this paper, we present UniFluids, a conditional flow-matching framework that harnesses the scalability of diffusion Transformer to unify learning of solution operators across diverse PDEs with varying dimensionality and physical variables. Unlike the autoregressive PDE foundation models, UniFluids adopts flow-matching to achieve parallel sequence generation, making it the first such approach for unified operator learning. Specifically, the introduction of a unified four-dimensional spatiotemporal representation for the heterogeneous PDE datasets enables joint training and conditional encoding. Furthermore, we find the effective dimension of the PDE dataset is much lower than its patch dimension. We thus employ $x$-prediction in the flow-matching operator learning, which is verified to significantly improve prediction accuracy. We conduct a large-scale evaluation of UniFluids on several PDE datasets covering spatial dimensions 1D, 2D and 3D. Experimental results show that UniFluids achieves strong prediction accuracy and demonstrates good scalability and cross-scenario generalization capability. The code will be released later.

</details>


### [189] [A Multi-Modal CNN-LSTM Framework with Multi-Head Attention and Focal Loss for Real-Time Elderly Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22313)
*Lijie Zhou,Luran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MultiModalFallDetector多模态深度学习框架，用于老年人实时跌倒检测，通过融合加速度计、陀螺仪和生理信号，结合多尺度CNN、注意力机制和焦点损失，在SisFall数据集上达到98.7% F1分数，推理延迟低于50ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化加剧了对可靠健康监测系统的需求，特别是老年人跌倒检测。传统基于单模态加速度数据的方法误报率高，而传统机器学习方法需要大量手工特征工程。

Method: 提出MultiModalFallDetector多模态深度学习框架，包含：多尺度CNN特征提取器捕捉不同时间分辨率运动动态；融合三轴加速度计、陀螺仪和四通道生理信号；多头部自注意力机制进行动态时间加权；采用Focal Loss缓解类别不平衡；引入辅助活动分类任务进行正则化；从UCI HAR到SisFall数据集实施迁移学习。

Result: 在SisFall数据集（包含60-85岁老年人真实模拟跌倒试验）上，框架达到F1分数98.7%、召回率98.9%、AUC-ROC 99.4%，显著优于传统机器学习和标准深度学习方法。模型在边缘设备上保持低于50ms的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 该多模态深度学习框架在老年人跌倒检测中表现出卓越性能，适合在老年护理环境中实时部署，为可靠的健康监测系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing global aging population has intensified the demand for reliable health monitoring systems, particularly those capable of detecting critical events such as falls among elderly individuals. Traditional fall detection approaches relying on single-modality acceleration data suffer from high false alarm rates, while conventional machine learning methods require extensive hand-crafted feature engineering. This paper proposes a novel multi-modal deep learning framework, MultiModalFallDetector, designed for real-time elderly fall detection using wearable sensors.
